**Statement** (stronger, for both). *Definition 17.4.3* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BMor⟧: a morphism $\Phi = (\bar\alpha, \phi, \bar\alpha')$ of $bC$ is a morphism $\phi : \alpha \to \alpha'$ of $C$ such that $h = (\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}}, \phi, \bar o'_{\mathrm{ref}})$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryRefTr⟧ carries the germ of $\bar\alpha$ to that of $\bar\alpha'$. ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.definition_17_4_3⟧: for representatives $t, t'$ of $\bar\alpha, \bar\alpha'$ this is the printed condition, that $(\bar o, \phi, \bar o') \in M(\Psi, \Psi')$ carries the germ of $\theta$ to that of $\theta'$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bLift_iff_tuple⟧; and given $\bar\alpha$ and $\phi$ with source $\alpha$ there is exactly one $\bar\alpha'$ with $(\bar\alpha, \phi, \bar\alpha')$ a morphism ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.existsUnique_bLift⟧. *Definition 17.4.4* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.definition_17_4_4⟧: these morphisms form a groupoid $bC$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryGroupoid⟧ with finite hom-sets, a groupoidal category ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryCategory⟧; $\bar\alpha \mapsto \alpha$, $\Phi \mapsto \phi$ is a functor $B_C : bC \to \partial C$ (17.63) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryFunctor⟧ into the full subcategory $\partial C$ of the objects with $d \ge 1$ ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldLevels · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.boundary⟧, and $B_C$ is faithful. Moreover, over every object $\alpha$ lie exactly $d(\alpha)$ objects of $bC$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.encard_obj_preimage⟧ (p. 791, "a finite number"). The composite with $\partial C \subset C$ is $B : bC \to C$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryFunctorC⟧.

**Proof of Definition 17.4.3.** *Step 1, the laws* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift.unit⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift.inv⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift.comp⟧: $1_\alpha$ lifts to $\bar\alpha \to \bar\alpha$ (its reference transition is the unit at $\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}}$); if $\phi$ lifts to $\bar\alpha \to \bar\alpha'$, $\phi^{-1}$ lifts back (the reference transition of $\phi^{-1}$ is the inverse of that of $\phi$); lifts compose (the reference transitions compose). Each is the corresponding law of transport. *Step 2, uniqueness* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift.unique⟧: two lifts of $\phi$ from $\bar\alpha$ have the same object $t(\phi)$ and germs that are both transports of the germ of $\bar\alpha$ by one $h$, hence equal. *Step 3, existence* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BLift.exists_of_src_eq⟧: $\bar\alpha' = (t(\phi), h_*p)$. *Step 4, the printed condition.* For representatives $t, t'$, the transition $(\bar o, \phi, \bar o')$ is the composite of the transport of $t$ to its reference, the reference transition of $\phi$, and the inverse of the transport of $t'$; the morphism is $1^{-1} \circ \phi \circ 1 = \phi$. By the composite and inverse laws, the reference condition holds iff the printed one does.

**Proof of Definition 17.4.4.** *Step 1, the groupoid.* Composition $(\bar\alpha', \phi', \bar\alpha'') \circ (\bar\alpha, \phi, \bar\alpha') = (\bar\alpha, \phi' \circ \phi, \bar\alpha'')$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BMor.compOf⟧, units $(\bar\alpha, 1_\alpha, \bar\alpha)$, inverses $(\bar\alpha', \phi^{-1}, \bar\alpha)$; the groupoid laws reduce to those of $C$, since a morphism of $bC$ is determined by its source and its morphism of $C$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BMor.ext_of_src⟧ (the target is then determined by Step 2 above). *Step 2, finite hom-sets.* $\Phi \mapsto \phi$ is injective on the morphisms $\bar\alpha \to \bar\beta$ (same source) and lands in the finite set of morphisms $\alpha \to \beta$ of $C$. *Step 3, over $\partial C$* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BObj.mem_boundarySet⟧: an object $(\alpha, p)$ has a face germ at $\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}}$, so $d_O(\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}}) \ge 1$, and $d(\alpha) = d_O(\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}})$ by Definition 17.1.30 ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldLevels · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.definition_17_1_30⟧. So $B_C$ is defined, a functor because composition in $\partial C$ is that of $C$; it is faithful by Step 1's determination by the source. *Step 4, the number of objects over $\alpha$.* The objects over $\alpha$ are in bijection with the face germs at $\bar o_{\mathrm{ref}}$, and a point $x$ of a tame M-polyfold carries exactly $d(x)$ face germs (Part II) ⟦Chapter2/TameBoundary · TameBoundary.encard_bMap_preimage⟧; with Definition 17.1.30 this is $d(\alpha)$.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* Two statements are stronger: the target $\bar\alpha'$ is determined by the source and $\phi$ (the text proves existence and uniqueness of $\bar\alpha'$ on p. 790; here it is the base of the groupoid structure), and $B_C$ is faithful. The count "a finite number of $\bar\alpha_i$" is made exact, $d(\alpha)$.

### XVII.38 Definition 17.4.5, Lemma 17.4.6 and (17.64) (new in the 2021 edition): the face uniformizers $\Psi_\theta$, p. 792; Remark 17.4.7, pp. 794–795

**Statement.** *Definition 17.4.5* (explicit) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceStabilizer⟧: for a face $\theta$, $G_\theta = \{g \in G : g\theta = \theta\}$. When $G$ fixes $\bar o$ ⟦Chapter17/LocalUniformizer · GroupoidalCategory.Uniformizer.FixesPoint⟧, $U(\bar o)$ is $G$-invariant ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.smul_mem⟧, $G$ permutes $F_{U(\bar o)}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.smul_mem_faces⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceAction⟧, and $G_\theta$ is the stabilizer of $\theta$ for this action ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.definition_17_4_5⟧. *(17.64)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj⟧: for $x \in \theta$, $\Psi_\theta(x)$ is the object of $bC$ over $\Psi(x)$ given by the face germ of $\theta$ at $x$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceGerm⟧; ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.display_17_64⟧: for every $\Psi'' \in F(\Psi(x))$, $\bar o''$ with $\Psi''(\bar o'') = \Psi(x)$ and good neighbourhood $U''(\bar o'')$, $(x, 1_{\Psi(x)}, \bar o'')$ carries $\theta$ to a uniquely determined face $\theta''$ of $U''(\bar o'')$, and $\Psi_\theta(x) = [\Psi(x), \Psi'', U''(\bar o''), \theta'']$. *Lemma 17.4.6* (narrower) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_6⟧: with $C$ tame, $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$, $\hat\Psi$ continuous for every uniformizer ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.HatContinuous⟧ and $G$ fixing $\bar o$, the functor $\Psi_\theta : G_\theta \ltimes \theta \to bC$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceFunctor⟧, $x \mapsto \Psi_\theta(x)$, $(g, x) \mapsto (\Psi_\theta(x), \Psi(g, x), \Psi_\theta(g x))$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceMor⟧, is injective on objects, full and faithful. Witnesses ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_tameBoundaryUniformizer_witness⟧: the quadrant polyfold is tame, has uniformizers, $\hat\Psi$ continuous ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_hatContinuous⟧ and is centred ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_isCentred⟧; over its corner lie two objects of $bC$, and a good neighbourhood of the corner has two faces.

*The hypotheses.* (1) *$G$ fixes $\bar o$.* Definition 17.1.3 does not ask for it; the text presumes it on p. 792 by calling $G$ "the isotropy group of $\alpha$", and the action of $G$ on $F_{U(\bar o)}$ needs it (point G.195). It enters as `hfix`, derived in the global statements of the later sections from `IsCentred`, every $\Psi \in F(\alpha)$ centred at $\alpha$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsCentred⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsCentred.fixesPoint⟧. Witnesses: the swap polyfold $\mathbb Z/2 \ltimes ([0, \infty)^2 \oplus W)$, $-1$ exchanging the two coordinates and so the two faces at the corner ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.swapConstruction⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.swapConstruction_isCentred⟧, not weak; and the quadrant, weak (trivial group). (2) *$\hat\Psi$ continuous* (`hc`). That $\Psi_\theta(g, x)$ is a morphism of $bC$ uses that the local sc-diffeomorphism of $\hat\Psi(g, x) \in M(\Psi, \Psi)$ is $g \ast \cdot$ near $x$, the conclusion of Lemma 17.1.9, which holds when $\hat\Psi$ is continuous ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_1_9_of_continuous⟧ and not as we read it in general (Findings, Chapter 17, item 1). Witnesses: the swap polyfold ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.swapConstruction_hatContinuous⟧, not weak, and the quadrant, weak.

**Proof of Definition 17.4.5 and the action.** *Step 1, the natural action is the group action* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.natRep_Φ_eq⟧: in $G \ltimes O$, for a natural representation at $\bar o$ and an isotropy element $(g, \bar o)$, the natural action is $y \mapsto g y$ on the (connected) neighbourhood: the $G$-component of the local section $\Gamma(g, \cdot)$ is continuous into the discrete $G$, hence constant, equal to $g$ at $\bar o$. *Step 2, invariance.* A good neighbourhood is a natural neighbourhood, so it is invariant under the natural action of the isotropy at $\bar o$; when $G$ fixes $\bar o$, every $g$ gives an isotropy element ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isoOf⟧, and Step 1 gives $g U(\bar o) = U(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.smul_U⟧. *Step 3, faces to faces.* $g \ast \cdot$ is an sc-diffeomorphism of $O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isScDiffeoOn_smul⟧, preserving $d_O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.degIndex_smul⟧, so it maps faces of $V(\bar o)$ to faces of $g V(\bar o) = V(\bar o)$. *Step 4.* The stabilizer of $\theta$ in $G$ is the stabilizer of the point $\theta$ of $F_{U(\bar o)}$; $G_\theta$ acts on $\theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceStabilizerAction⟧.

**Objects over the points of a uniformizer.** *Step 1, $\mathrm{pt}(p)$* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ptObj⟧: a face germ $p$ at $x \in \partial O$ gives the object $(\Psi(x), h_*p)$ of $bC$, with $h = (x, 1_{\Psi(x)}, \bar o_{\mathrm{ref}})$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ptTr⟧. *Step 2, morphisms* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bLift_ptObj_iff⟧: for $h \in M(\Psi, \Psi')$ with $s(h)$, $t(h)$ the base points of $p$, $p'$, the morphism $h.\phi$ lifts to $\mathrm{pt}(p) \to \mathrm{pt}(p')$ iff $h$ carries $p$ to $p'$; as in the proof of Definition 17.4.3, by composing with the transports to the references and their inverses. *Step 3, injectivity* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ptObj_injective⟧: equal objects lie over one object, so the base points agree ($\Psi$ is injective on objects), and the unit lifts between them; by Step 2 the unit carries $p$ to $p'$, so $p = p'$.

**Face germs of a face.** *Step 1* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.phi_bijective⟧: Part II's map $\Phi_{(\bar o, U(\bar o), \theta)}$ from the germs near $[\bar o, U(\bar o), \theta]$ to $\theta$ is bijective, because $U(\bar o)$ is centred at $\bar o$ ⟦Chapter2/TameBoundary · TameBoundary.phi_bijective_of_isCenteredAt⟧; its inverse is the face germ of $\theta$ at $x$, a germ at $x$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.bMap_faceGerm⟧, equal to $[\bar o, U(\bar o), \theta]$ at $\bar o$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceGerm_self⟧. *Step 2* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.eq_of_faceGerm_eq⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_faceGerm_eq⟧: distinct faces through $x$ have distinct germs at $x$, and every face germ at a point $x \in U(\bar o)$ is the germ of a face of $U(\bar o)$ through $x$. *Step 3* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isLiftOf_smul_faceGerm⟧: when $G$ fixes $\bar o$, $g \ast \cdot$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to the germ of $g\theta$ at $g x$: the image under $g$ of a small triple at $x$ inside $\theta$ is a small triple at $g x$ inside $g\theta$, because $g$ maps $U(\bar o)$ onto itself and fixes $\bar o$, and extensions of faces commute with sc-diffeomorphisms.

**$\hat\Psi$ and germs.** ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.germMap_hat_eventuallyEq⟧: if $\hat\Psi$ is continuous ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.hat⟧, the local sc-diffeomorphism of $\hat\Psi(g, x) = (x, \Psi(g, x), g x)$ is $g \ast \cdot$ near $x$: the continuous section $y \mapsto \hat\Psi(g, y)$ of $s$ through $\hat\Psi(g, x)$ agrees near $x$ with the local inverse of $s$ of any admissible triple, and its target is $g y$. Hence $\hat\Psi(g, x)$ carries $p$ to $q$ iff $g \ast \cdot$ does ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.germLift_hat_iff⟧, and in particular carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to the germ of $g\theta$ at $g x$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.germLift_hat_faceGerm⟧. The morphism $(\Psi_\theta(x), \Psi(g, x), \Psi_{g\theta}(g x))$ of $bC$ is therefore defined for every $g \in G$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.smulMor⟧, and for $g \in G_\theta$ it is $\Psi_\theta(g, x)$.

**The core lemma** ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_smul_of_bLift⟧. A morphism $\phi$ of $C$ that lifts to $\Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi_{\theta'}(x')$ is $\Psi(g, x)$ for some $g$ with $g x = x'$ and $g\theta = \theta'$. *Step 1.* $\phi : \Psi(x) \to \Psi(x')$, so $\phi = \Psi(g, x)$ with $g x = x'$ by fullness of $\Psi$. *Step 2.* By the morphisms of $\mathrm{pt}$, $\hat\Psi(g, x)$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to that of $\theta'$ at $x'$; by $\hat\Psi$ and germs it also carries it to that of $g\theta$ at $g x$. *Step 3.* Uniqueness of transport makes the two germs at $x'$ equal, and distinct faces through $x'$ have distinct germs: $g\theta = \theta'$.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.6.** *Functor.* $\Psi_\theta$ respects composition and units because $\Psi$ does and morphisms of $bC$ are determined by source and morphism of $C$. *Injective on objects.* $\Psi_\theta(x) = \Psi_{\theta'}(x')$ forces $x = x'$ and $\theta = \theta'$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj_inj⟧: $\mathrm{pt}$ is injective, so the germs agree, hence the base points, hence the faces. *Full.* A morphism $\Phi : \Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi_\theta(x')$ has $\Phi.\phi = \Psi(g, x)$ with $g x = x'$ and $g\theta = \theta$ by the core lemma, so $g \in G_\theta$ and $\Phi = \Psi_\theta(g, x)$. *Faithful.* $\Psi(g, x) = \Psi(g', x)$ forces $g = g'$ ⟦Chapter17/LocalUniformizer · GroupoidalCategory.Uniformizer.mor_injective⟧.

**Proof of (17.64) with the book's choices.** *Step 1* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BdryTuple.toBObj_eq_ptObj⟧: a tuple $t$ is the object $\mathrm{pt}$ of its own germ. *Step 2* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj_eq_toBObj⟧: if $(x, 1_{\Psi(x)}, \bar o'')$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to the germ of the tuple $t$, then by the morphisms of $\mathrm{pt}$ the unit $1_{\Psi(x)}$ lifts from $\Psi_\theta(x)$ to $t$, and the unit also lifts from $\Psi_\theta(x)$ to itself; uniqueness of lifts gives $\Psi_\theta(x) = [t]$. *Step 3.* For $\Psi''$, $\bar o''$, $U''(\bar o'')$ given, transport the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ by $(x, 1, \bar o'')$; the result is a germ at $\bar o''$, so the germ of a face $\theta''$ of $U''(\bar o'')$, unique since faces of $U''(\bar o'')$ have distinct germs at $\bar o''$; Step 2 concludes.

*Remark 17.4.7* (pp. 794–795, (17.66); nothing to formalize). The remark motivates the construction by the configuration of faces and the functor diagrams (17.66) and describes applications; it makes no mathematical assertion (point G.213).

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The verdict of Lemma 17.4.6 is narrower: it is proved with $\hat\Psi$ continuous and $G$ fixing $\bar o$. Fullness is proved from the $G$-invariance of $U(\bar o)$ and the distinctness of face germs, instead of the printed open–closed argument in $\theta^\circ$: $g\theta$ and $\theta$ are faces of $U(\bar o)$ with the same germ at $g x$, hence equal.

### XVII.39 Lemmas 17.4.8 and 17.4.9 (new in the 2021 edition): the images of the $\Psi_\theta$ and the functor $\bar\Psi$, (17.65)–(17.69), pp. 795–797

**Statement** (narrower, for both; hypotheses as in Lemma 17.4.6: $C$ tame, $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$, $\hat\Psi$ continuous, $G$ fixing $\bar o$). *Lemma 17.4.8* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_8⟧: for faces $\theta, \theta'$ of $U(\bar o)$, the images $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|, |\Psi_{\theta'}(\theta')| \subset |bC|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceImage⟧ are equal or disjoint, and equal iff $g\theta = \theta'$ for some $g \in G$. *(17.65) and Lemma 17.4.9* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_9⟧: on the pairs $(x, \theta)$ with $x \in \theta \in F_{U(\bar o)}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.FacePt⟧, $\bar\Psi(x, \theta) = \Psi_\theta(x)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.barObj⟧ is injective; the morphisms $\bar\Psi(g, (x, \theta)) = (\Psi_\theta(x), \Psi(g, x), \Psi_{g\theta}(g x))$ are determined by $g$; and every morphism $\bar\Psi(x, \theta) \to \bar\Psi(x', \theta')$ of $bC$ is $\bar\Psi(g, (x, \theta))$ with $g x = x'$, $g\theta = \theta'$. *(17.67)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.barOrbitMap⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.barOrbitMap_injective⟧: the induced map $|\bar\Psi| : G \backslash \bigsqcup_\theta \theta \to |bC|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.barSetoid⟧ is injective. *(17.68)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.display_17_68⟧: its image is $|B|^{-1}(|\Psi(U(\bar o))|)$. *(17.69)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.display_17_69⟧: $|\bar\Psi|$ is a bijection of $G \backslash \bigsqcup_\theta \theta$ onto that set, and on one face the orbits are those of $G_\theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.barSetoid_r_iff_of_eq⟧, so $G \backslash \bigsqcup_\theta \theta = \bigsqcup_i |\theta_i|$ as sets.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.8.** *Step 1, one inclusion* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceImage_subset⟧: if $g\theta = \theta'$, then for $x \in \theta$ the morphism $\bar\Psi(g, (x, \theta))$ connects $\Psi_\theta(x)$ with $\Psi_{\theta'}(g x)$, so $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)| \subset |\Psi_{\theta'}(\theta')|$; with $g^{-1}$ the other inclusion. *Step 2, a common point gives $g$.* If $|\Psi_\theta(x)| = |\Psi_{\theta'}(x')|$, a morphism $\Phi$ of $bC$ connects them, and the core lemma gives $g$ with $g\theta = \theta'$. *Step 3, the iff.* If the images are equal, $\bar o \in \theta$ gives a point of $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$, which lies in $|\Psi_{\theta'}(\theta')|$; Step 2 gives $g$. The converse is Step 1. *Step 4, the dichotomy.* If the images meet, Steps 2 and 1 make them equal; otherwise they are disjoint.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.9.** *Injective* by the injectivity of $\Psi_\theta(x)$ in $(x, \theta)$. *Determined by $g$* by injectivity of $\Psi$ on morphisms. *Full* by the core lemma, the morphism being determined by its source and its morphism of $C$.

**Proof of (17.67)–(17.69).** *Step 1, the orbits.* $(x, \theta) \sim (x', \theta')$ iff $g x = x'$, $g\theta = \theta'$ for some $g$; on one face this is the action of $G_\theta$. *Step 2, (17.67).* $|\bar\Psi|$ is well defined, via the morphisms $\bar\Psi(g, \cdot)$; injective, since a morphism of $bC$ between $\bar\Psi(x, \theta)$ and $\bar\Psi(x', \theta')$ comes from some $g$ by the core lemma. *Step 3, (17.68), "$\subset$".* $\bar\Psi(x, \theta)$ lies over $\Psi(x)$ with $x \in \theta \subset U(\bar o)$. *"$\supset$".* Let $\bar a$ be an object of $bC$ whose base $a$ is isomorphic to $\Psi(y)$, $y \in U(\bar o)$, through $\phi$. Lift $\phi$ to $\bar a \to \bar b$; $\bar b$ lies over $\Psi(y)$, so it is $\mathrm{pt}(q)$ for a face germ $q$ at $y$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_ptObj_eq⟧ (transport the germ of $\bar b$ from the reference to $y$ by the unit, and use uniqueness of lifts); $q$ is the germ of a face $\theta$ of $U(\bar o)$ through $y$; so $|\bar a| = |\bar\Psi(y, \theta)|$. *Step 4, (17.69).* Steps 2 and 3 give the bijection.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The groupoid $G \ltimes \bigsqcup_\theta \theta$ is not built; Lemma 17.4.9 is stated for the object map $\bar\Psi$ and the morphisms $\bar\Psi(g, \cdot)$. Lemma 17.4.8 is sharpened: the images agree exactly when some $g$ maps $\theta$ to $\theta'$. In (17.69) the "obvious homeomorphism" $\bigsqcup_i |\theta_i| \to G \backslash \bigsqcup_\theta \theta$ is used as an identification of sets; the topology of $|bC|$ comes later, with Definition 17.4.17 (point G.214).

### XVII.40 Lemmas 17.4.10, 17.4.11 and Definition 17.4.12 (new in the 2021 edition): face transition sets, (17.70), pp. 797–798

In this section and the next two, $\hat\Psi$ continuity and the centring of $G$ are not assumed: only $C$ tame and $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$. We take uniformizers $\Psi, \Psi'$, points $\bar o, \bar o'$ with good neighbourhoods $U(\bar o)$, $U'(\bar o')$, and faces $\theta \in F_{U(\bar o)}$, $\theta' \in F_{U'(\bar o')}$.

**Statement** (same, for all three). *The transition set $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$* (p. 797) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.FaceTr⟧: the triples $(x, \Phi, x')$ with $x \in \theta$, $x' \in \theta'$ and $\Phi : \Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi'_{\theta'}(x')$ a morphism of $bC$, mapped to $(x, \phi, x') \in M(\Psi, \Psi')$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrToTr⟧. *Lemma 17.4.10* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_10⟧: (1) for $(x, \Phi, x') \in M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$, $h = (x, \phi, x')$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to the germ of $\theta'$ at $x'$; (2) conversely, for $h = (x, \phi, x') \in M(\Psi, \Psi')$ with $x \in \theta$, $x' \in \theta'$ and this property, $\Phi = (\Psi_\theta(x), \phi, \Psi'_{\theta'}(x'))$ is a morphism of $bC$ and $(x, \Phi, x')$ is an element of $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ over $h$. *Lemma 17.4.11* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_11⟧: if $M(\Psi, \Psi')_{U, U'} = \{(q, \phi, q') : q \in U(\bar o), q' \in U'(\bar o')\}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.trUU⟧ is empty, every $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ is empty. *Definition 17.4.12* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.transFaceSet⟧: $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is the set of $h = (x, \phi, x')$ with $x \in \theta$, $x' \in \theta'$ and $h_*\theta = \theta'$, read on germs. ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.definition_17_4_12⟧: (17.70) $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'} \subset M(\Psi, \Psi')_{U, U'}$; every $h \in M(\Psi, \Psi')_{U, U'}$ with source in $\theta$ lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ for some face $\theta'$, and for only one; and $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is the image of $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.10.** (1) $\Phi$ is a morphism $\mathrm{pt}(\text{germ of } \theta \text{ at } x) \to \mathrm{pt}(\text{germ of } \theta' \text{ at } x')$, so by the morphisms of $\mathrm{pt}$ (previous sections) $h$ carries the one germ to the other. (2) The same equivalence the other way gives that $\phi$ lifts; the element is $((x), (\Psi_\theta(x), \phi, \Psi'_{\theta'}(x')), (x'))$, and it maps to $h$.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.11.** An element of $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ maps to an element of $M(\Psi, \Psi')$ with source in $\theta \subset U(\bar o)$ and target in $\theta' \subset U'(\bar o')$, so in $M(\Psi, \Psi')_{U, U'}$.

**Proof of Definition 17.4.12.** *Step 1, (17.70)*: faces lie in their good neighbourhoods. *Step 2, existence of $\theta'$.* For $h \in M(\Psi, \Psi')_{U, U'}$ with $x = s(h) \in \theta$, transport the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ by $h$; the result is a germ at $t(h) \in U'(\bar o')$, so the germ of a face $\theta'$ of $U'(\bar o')$ through $t(h)$. *Step 3, uniqueness*: two such faces have equal germs at $t(h)$ (uniqueness of transport), hence are equal. *Step 4, the image*: Lemma 17.4.10 (1) gives "$\subset$", (2) gives "$\supset$".

*Where Lean departs from the text.* None; "$h_*\theta = \theta'$" is read on germs, as in Definition 17.4.2.

### XVII.41 Lemma 17.4.13 (new in the 2021 edition): $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is a tame sub-M-polyfold, (17.71)–(17.73), pp. 798–800

**Statement** (restated). ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_13⟧: for $C$ tame with $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$, good neighbourhoods and faces as before, (1) the induced structure on $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is tame in the sense of Definition 2.5.18 ⟦Chapter2/TameBoundary · MPolyfold.HasTameInducedStructure⟧, and so is that of the face $\theta$; (2) $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is a sub-M-polyfold of $M(\Psi, \Psi')$, of the form $\mathcal O \cap s^{-1}(\theta)$ with $\mathcal O$ open; (3) near every element, $s$ is an sc-diffeomorphism $U \to N$ carrying $U \cap M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ onto $N \cap \theta$ (the map $R$ of (17.73)); (4) on it the degeneracy index of $M(\Psi, \Psi')$ is $d_O \circ s \ge 1$; (5) (17.71): $(x, \Phi, x') \mapsto (x, \phi, x')$ is injective from $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ with image $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrEquiv⟧. The form without the tameness clause ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_13_subMPolyfold⟧. *The literal form* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_13_literal⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_13_literal_quadrant⟧: with "tame sub-M-polyfold" in the sense of Definition 2.5.19 ⟦Chapter2/TameBoundary · MPolyfold.IsTameSubMPolyfold⟧, the assertion is not true for every tame polyfold structure; on the quadrant, at the unit over the corner, $M(\Psi, \Psi)^*_{\theta, \theta}$ is not a tame sub-M-polyfold in that sense.

*Reading.* We read the faces in the sense of Definition 2.5.18, the sense the book gives faces in Proposition 2.5.15 – Theorem 2.5.17; the tameness asserted is that of the induced structure, which is what the construction of $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ uses. The reason for the reading is the literal form: the text points to Definition 2.5.19 before the lemma, whose condition (2) asks for a retraction preserving the degeneracy index; $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ lies in $\{d \ge 1\}$ and near the unit at a corner meets the closure of $\{d = 0\}$, so no such retraction exists. Under Definition 2.5.19 no face of positive codimension would qualify (Findings, Chapter 17, item 6).

**Proof of (2), (3), (4).** *Step 1, local constancy of transport* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_nhds_germLift⟧. Let $h$ carry the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to the germ of $\theta'$ at $x'$, and let $T$ be an admissible triple of $h$ with local inverse $\sigma$ of $s$ and $f = t \circ \sigma$. Then there is an open $N \ni x$ inside the domain of $\sigma$ such that for every $y \in \theta \cap N$, $f(y) \in \theta'$ and $\sigma(y)$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $y$ to the germ of $\theta'$ at $f(y)$. The germs of $\theta$ at $x$ and of $\theta'$ at $x'$ are represented by small triples $(x, V_s, \theta_s)$, $(x', V_{s'}, \theta_{s'})$, and the lift at $x$ by a triple $(x, W, \theta_t)$ on which $f$ maps $\theta_t$ to $\theta_{s'}$; on the intersection $N$ of these neighbourhoods with $f^{-1}(V_{s'})$ the same triples represent the germs at every point $y$, and $f$ is the local sc-diffeomorphism of $\sigma(y)$ there.

*Step 2, openness in $s^{-1}(\theta)$* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_isOpen_transFaceSet⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_isOpen_inter_eq⟧: by Step 1, every element of the carrier $W(h)$ of $T$ with source in $N \cap \theta$ lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ (it is $\sigma$ of its source). Taking $\mathcal O$ the union over all elements $h$ of $W(h) \cap s^{-1}(N_h)$ gives $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'} = \mathcal O \cap s^{-1}(\theta)$.

*Step 3, the face is a sub-M-polyfold* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isSubMPolyfold_face⟧: $\theta$ is a face of the face-structured open set $V(\bar o)$ of the tame $O$, a sub-M-polyfold by Theorem 2.5.17 (Part II) ⟦Chapter2/TameBoundaryStructure · MPolyfold.isSubMPolyfold_of_isFaceIn⟧.

*Step 4, (2)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isSubMPolyfold_transFaceSet⟧: $s^{-1}(\theta)$ is a sub-M-polyfold (preimage under the local sc-diffeomorphism $s$), and its intersection with the open $\mathcal O$ is one ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · MPolyfold.IsSubMPolyfold.inter_isOpen⟧.

*Step 5, (3)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_sheet_transFaceSet⟧: with $T$ and $N$ of Step 2 at $h$, put $U = W(h) \cap s^{-1}(N)$; $s : U \to N$ is an sc-diffeomorphism with inverse $\sigma$, and $s(U \cap M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}) = N \cap \theta$ by Step 2. The map $R = (s|W(h_0))^{-1} \circ r \circ s$ of the text is this sheet composed with a retraction onto $\theta$.

*Step 6, (4)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.degIndex_tr⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.one_le_degIndex_of_mem_transFaceSet⟧: a local sc-diffeomorphism preserves the degeneracy index, so $d_{M(\Psi, \Psi')} = d_O \circ s$, which is $\ge 1$ on $s^{-1}(\theta)$ since $\theta \subset \partial O$.

**Proof of (5).** *Injective* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrToTr_injective⟧: equal images have equal source and target points and equal morphisms of $C$; the morphism of $bC$ is determined by its source and its morphism of $C$. *Image*: Definition 17.4.12.

**Proof of (1).** *Step 1, faces* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · MPolyfold.hasTameInducedStructure_of_isFaceIn⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.hasTameInducedStructure_face⟧: a face of a face-structured open subset $V$ of a tame M-polyfold has a tame induced structure. Restrict the M-polyfold to the open set $V$, which is tame and face-structured; the face corresponds to a face of the restriction; Theorem 2.5.17 makes that face tame with its induced structure ⟦Chapter2/TameFaces · MPolyfold.theorem_2_5_17_isTame_face⟧; and the inclusion of $V$ is an sc-diffeomorphism onto an open set, which carries tame induced structures ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · MPolyfold.HasTameInducedStructure.image_of_isScDiffeoOn_of_isSubMPolyfold⟧. *Step 2, open pieces* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · MPolyfold.HasTameInducedStructure.inter_isOpen_of_isSubMPolyfold⟧: $N \cap \theta$ has a tame induced structure for $N$ open. *Step 3, locality* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · MPolyfold.hasTameInducedStructure_of_local⟧: a sub-M-polyfold whose intersection with an open neighbourhood of each of its points has a tame induced structure has one. *Step 4* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.hasTameInducedStructure_transFaceSet⟧: near an element, the sheet $\sigma : N \to U$ of (3) is an sc-diffeomorphism carrying $N \cap \theta$ onto $U \cap M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$; by Steps 1–2 the source has a tame induced structure, by Step 1's transport so does the image, and Step 3 concludes.

**Proof of the literal form.** *Step 1, a general obstruction* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · MPolyfold.not_isTameSubMPolyfold_of_mem_closure⟧: a set $Y$ inside $\{d \ge 1\}$ with a point $y$ in the closure of $\{d = 0\}$ is not a tame sub-M-polyfold in the sense of Definition 2.5.19: a tame retraction $r$ near $y$ has $d(r(x)) = d(x)$, and a point $x$ of index $0$ near $y$ would give $r(x) \in Y$ of index $0$. *Step 2, the quadrant.* In the quadrant polyfold take a good neighbourhood of the corner and one of its two faces $\theta$; the unit $(0, 1, 0)$ lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi)^*_{\theta, \theta}$, since units preserve germs. *Step 3.* The corner is a limit of interior points of $[0, \infty)^2 \oplus W$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · TameBoundaryExample.corner_mem_closure_dq_eq_zero⟧, and a local sc-diffeomorphism pulls back the closure of $\{d = 0\}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · MPolyfold.IsLocalScDiffeo.mem_closure_degIndex_eq_zero⟧; so the unit is in the closure of $\{d = 0\}$ in $M(\Psi, \Psi)$. With (4), Step 1 applies. *Step 4.* The universal statement over all tame polyfold structures at universe $0$ does not hold at this example, with $W = 0$.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The proof in the text builds the retraction $R = (s|W(h_0))^{-1} \circ r \circ s$ from a tame retraction $r$ onto $\theta$ inside $V(\bar o)$, with $d_\theta$ in place of $d_O$, which is the induced tameness of Definition 2.5.18; the formalization splits the argument into the sub-M-polyfold part (sheets of $s$ over $\theta$) and the tameness part (transport of the induced tameness of $\theta$ along the sheets). The key step the text leaves implicit is the local constancy of transport (Step 1 of (2)), which makes $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ open in $s^{-1}(\theta)$.

### XVII.42 Definition 17.4.14, Lemma 17.4.15 and Theorem 17.4.16 (new in the 2021 edition): the M-polyfolds $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ and their structure maps, (17.74), pp. 800–801

**Statement** (same, for all three). *The face as an M-polyfold* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceSubPolyfold⟧: $\theta$ with its structure as a sub-M-polyfold of $O$ (Theorem 2.5.17); it is tame ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isTame_faceSubPolyfold⟧. *Definition 17.4.14* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.FTr⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftrHomeomorph⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftrPolyfold⟧: $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ carries the topology and the M-polyfold structure for which (17.74), the bijection of Lemma 17.4.13 (5), is an sc-diffeomorphism onto the sub-M-polyfold $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScDiffeoOn_homeomorph⟧; this M-polyfold is tame ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isTame_ftrPolyfold⟧. *Lemma 17.4.15* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_15⟧: for this structure, $s : M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'}) \to \theta$ and $t : M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'}) \to \theta'$ are local sc-diffeomorphisms, the inversion $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'}) \to M(\Psi'_{\theta'}, \Psi_\theta)$ is an sc-diffeomorphism, and the unit map $\theta \to M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi_\theta)$ and the multiplication on the fibred product are sc-smooth. *Theorem 17.4.16* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.theorem_17_4_16⟧: the same for all uniformizers, good neighbourhoods and faces at once.

**Proof of Definition 17.4.14.** *Step 1.* The topology on $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$ is the one induced by the bijection (17.74), which is then a homeomorphism onto $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$; Hausdorff and paracompact follow. *Step 2.* The M-polyfold structure is the sub-M-polyfold structure of $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ (Lemma 17.4.13 (2)) transported along the inverse homeomorphism; a transported structure makes the homeomorphism an sc-diffeomorphism ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isLocalScDiffeo_homeomorph⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScSmoothMap_homeomorph_symm⟧. "Uniquely determined" in the text is this transport. *Step 3, tameness.* The induced structure of $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ is tame (Lemma 17.4.13 (1)), and tameness passes along the local sc-diffeomorphism (17.74).

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.15.** *Step 1, restriction of local sc-diffeomorphisms* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · MPolyfold.isLocalScDiffeo_subPolyfold_of_inter⟧: if $f$ is a local sc-diffeomorphism, $A$ a sub-M-polyfold of the target, $B$ one of the source, $\mathcal O$ open with $B \subset \mathcal O$ and $x \in B \iff f(x) \in A$ for $x \in \mathcal O$, then $f : B \to A$ is a local sc-diffeomorphism of the induced structures: the local inverses of $f$, restricted to the open set $W_0 \cap \mathcal O$, map $A$ into $B$ there.

*Step 2, the source.* Apply Step 1 to $s : M(\Psi, \Psi') \to O$ with $A = \theta$, $B = M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ and $\mathcal O$ of Lemma 17.4.13 (2), where $B = \mathcal O \cap s^{-1}(\theta)$; compose with (17.74) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isLocalScDiffeo_src⟧.

*Step 3, the target.* $h \in M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ iff $h^{-1} \in M(\Psi', \Psi)^*_{\theta', \theta}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.inv_mem_transFaceSet_iff⟧, by the inverse law of transport. Since the inversion of $M(\Psi, \Psi')$ is continuous ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isScSmoothMap_trInv⟧, the preimage of the open set of Lemma 17.4.13 (2) for $(\Psi', \Psi)$ gives $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'} = \mathcal O' \cap t^{-1}(\theta')$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_isOpen_inter_eq_tgt⟧, and Step 1 applies to $t$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isLocalScDiffeo_tgt⟧.

*Step 4, the inversion* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScSmoothMap_inv⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScDiffeoOn_inv⟧: (17.74) commutes with inversion ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrToTr_inv⟧, the ambient inversion is sc-smooth and maps $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ into $M(\Psi', \Psi)^*_{\theta', \theta}$ (Step 3), so the inversion is sc-smooth between the induced structures; it is its own inverse, hence an sc-diffeomorphism.

*Step 5, the unit* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScSmoothMap_unit⟧: (17.74) carries units to units ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrToTr_unit⟧; the unit $(q, 1, q)$ lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi)^*_{\theta, \theta}$ for $q \in \theta$ (units preserve germs); and the ambient unit map $O \to M(\Psi, \Psi)$ ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isScSmoothMap_trUnit⟧, composed with the inclusion of $\theta$, is sc-smooth into the induced structure.

*Step 6, the multiplication* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftr_isScSmoothMap_mul⟧: composites of elements of $M(\Psi', \Psi'')^*_{\theta', \theta''}$ and $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$ lie in $M(\Psi, \Psi'')^*_{\theta, \theta''}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.mul_mem_transFaceSet⟧, by the composite law of transport (the germ of $\theta'$ at the common point is the same from both sides). The fibred product of the face transition sets maps sc-smoothly into the ambient fibred product (components: (17.74) followed by inclusions of sub-M-polyfolds), the ambient multiplication is sc-smooth, its values lie in $M(\Psi, \Psi'')^*_{\theta, \theta''}$, and (17.74) commutes with composition ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceTrToTr_comp⟧; so the multiplication is sc-smooth for the transported structures. The argument is the one of Proposition 17.3.6, Step 3 (c).

**Proof of Theorem 17.4.16.** Lemma 17.4.15 for every choice of $\Psi, \Psi', \Psi''$, points, good neighbourhoods and faces.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* None. The target of $s$ is the face $\theta$ with its structure as a sub-M-polyfold of $O$, the M-polyfold of objects of $\Psi_\theta$ (p. 801). The hypotheses ($C$ tame, $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$) are met non-degenerately by the quadrant polyfold, where the unit at the corner lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi)^*_{\theta, \theta}$ for both faces $\theta$; tameness of all these M-polyfolds, used in Theorem 17.4.26, is Definition 17.4.14, Step 3.

### XVII.43 Definition 17.4.17 and Lemma 17.4.18 (new in the 2021 edition), pp. 802–803: the topology $T_{bC}$ on $|bC|$

Throughout this and the following sections, $(C, F, M)$ is a polyfold structure on a GCT which is tame ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsTame⟧ and has $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$ for every object ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.HasUniformizers⟧, the standing reading of §17.1 (point G.196). The tame boundary category $bC$ is the groupoid of Definitions 17.4.2–17.4.4 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryGroupoid⟧, whose objects are modelled through one reference uniformizer per object ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BObj⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryRefUnif⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.refPt⟧, and $|bC|$ is its orbit space ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BOrbit⟧. For a uniformizer $\Psi : G \ltimes O \to C$, a point $\bar o \in \partial O$, a good open neighbourhood $U(\bar o) = V(\bar o) \cap \partial O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd⟧ and a face $\theta$ of $U(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faces⟧, $\Psi_\theta(x)$, $x \in \theta$, is the object of $bC$ given by the face germ of $\theta$ at $x$ (17.64) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj⟧; it lies over $\Psi(x)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj_α⟧, and $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ is the class $[\alpha, \Psi, U(\bar o), \theta]$ of Definition 17.4.2 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj_center⟧.

**Statement** (restated; Lemma 17.4.18 same). *Definition 17.4.17* (p. 802) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsBdryOpen⟧: a set $Q \subset |bC|$ is open if every $z \in Q$ has a representative $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$, $|\Psi_\theta(\bar o)| = z$, and an open set $A \subset O$ containing $\bar o$ with $|\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)| \subset Q$. Here $|\Psi_\theta(A)| = \{|\Psi_\theta(x)| : x \in A \cap \theta\}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceImageOn⟧. *Lemma 17.4.18* (p. 803) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_18⟧: these sets form a topology $T_{bC}$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryTopology⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isOpen_bdry_iff⟧, and for every good neighbourhood, face $\theta$ and open $A \subset O$, the set $|\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)|$ is open ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isOpen_faceImageOn⟧.

*Readings.* (1) The text requires the condition "for every $z \in |bC|$". We read "for every $z \in Q$", as the proof of Lemma 17.4.18 does: it says that $\emptyset$ clearly belongs to the collection, and it begins the intersection argument with a point of $Q \cap Q'$. The printed form is kept as a statement ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsBdryOpenLiteral⟧, and under it the empty set is not open as soon as $bC$ has an object ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.not_isBdryOpenLiteral_empty⟧, already for the quadrant polyfold ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_18_literal⟧. (2) "An open $A(\bar o) \subset \theta$" is read as $A \cap \theta$ with $A$ open in $O$, so that $A \cap \theta$ is an open neighbourhood of $\bar o$ in $\theta$ with its subspace topology (Findings, Chapter 17, item 7). No continuity of $\hat\Psi$ and no centring of the uniformizers is needed in this section.

**Proof of the key local step: openness moves from one representative to another.** Everything in this section rests on one statement, the level-$|bC|$ form of the local sc-diffeomorphism $\bar f$ that the text uses on p. 802 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_nhds_π_eq⟧: *if $\Psi_\theta(x)$ and $\Psi'_{\theta'}(x')$ are isomorphic in $bC$, then for every open $A' \ni x'$ in $O'$ there is an open $N \ni x$ in $O$ such that every $\Psi_\theta(y)$, $y \in N \cap \theta$, is isomorphic to some $\Psi'_{\theta'}(y')$ with $y' \in A' \cap \theta'$.*

*Step 1, a transition element.* An isomorphism $\Phi : \Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi'_{\theta'}(x')$ in $bC$ is a morphism of the face transition set $M(\Psi_\theta, \Psi'_{\theta'})$; its image in $M(\Psi, \Psi')$ is an element $a = (x, \varphi, x')$ of $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta, \theta'}$, by the description of $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$ as the image of the face transition set in Definition 17.4.12 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.definition_17_4_12⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_mem_transFaceSet_of_π_eq⟧.

*Step 2, $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$ is open in $s^{-1}(\theta)$ near $a$.* This is the part of Lemma 17.4.13 that the topology needs ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_isOpen_transFaceSet⟧: there are an admissible triple $T$ at $a$ ⟦Chapter17/PolyfoldConstruction · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple⟧ and an open $N_0 \ni x$ inside the source set of $T$ such that every element $b$ of the carrier of $T$ with $s(b) \in N_0 \cap \theta$ lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$. The proof carries the face germs along: if $a$ carries the germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to that of $\theta'$ at $x'$, then near $x$ each $(s|_T)^{-1}(y)$, $y \in \theta$, carries the germ of $\theta$ at $y$ to that of $\theta'$ at its target ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransition · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_nhds_germLift⟧.

*Step 3, the local sc-diffeomorphism.* Let $f = t \circ (s|_T)^{-1}$ be the local sc-diffeomorphism of the triple ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryGerms · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple.germMap⟧; it is a degeneracy-preserving homeomorphism on the source set ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryGerms · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple.isDHomeoOn_germMap⟧ with $f(x) = x'$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryGerms · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple.germMap_src⟧. Take $N = N_0 \cap \mathrm{src}(T) \cap f^{-1}(A')$, open by continuity of $f$, containing $x$.

*Step 4, conclusion.* For $y \in N \cap \theta$, the element $b = (s|_T)^{-1}(y)$ lies in the carrier ⟦Chapter17/AdmissibleTripleBasic · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple.srcInv_mem⟧ with $s(b) = y$ ⟦Chapter17/AdmissibleTripleBasic · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.AdmissibleTriple.src_srcInv⟧, so by Step 2 it lies in $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$. By Lemma 17.4.10 an element of that set gives a morphism of $bC$ from $\Psi_\theta(y)$ to $\Psi'_{\theta'}(t(b))$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_10⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.π_eq_of_mem_transFaceSet⟧, and $y' = t(b) = f(y) \in A' \cap \theta'$.

**Corollary: openness at every point of a face** ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsBdryOpen.exists_nhds⟧. If $Q$ is open and $|\Psi_\theta(x)| \in Q$ for some $x \in \theta$ (not only $x = \bar o$), there is an open $N \ni x$ with $|\Psi_\theta(N \cap \theta)| \subset Q$. *Proof.* Definition 17.4.17 gives a representative $\Psi'_{\theta'}(\bar o')$ of $|\Psi_\theta(x)|$ and an open $A \ni \bar o'$ with $|\Psi'_{\theta'}(A \cap \theta')| \subset Q$; the key step with the roles of the two representatives as stated gives $N$, and each $|\Psi_\theta(y)|$, $y \in N \cap \theta$, equals some $|\Psi'_{\theta'}(y')|$ with $y' \in A \cap \theta'$, hence lies in $Q$.

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.18.** *Step 1, every point has a representative.* Every object of $bC$ is the class of a tuple $(\alpha, \Psi, U(\bar o), \theta)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BdryTuple.toBObj_surjective⟧, hence of the form $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_faceObj_center_eq⟧.

*Step 2, $|bC|$ is open*: for $z$ take the representative of Step 1 and $A = O$. *$\emptyset$ is open* vacuously, under the reading $z \in Q$.

*Step 3, intersections.* For $z \in Q \cap Q'$, the openness of $Q$ gives a representative $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ and $A$; the corollary above, applied to $Q'$ at the point $\bar o$ of this same face, gives an open $N \ni \bar o$ with $|\Psi_\theta(N \cap \theta)| \subset Q'$. Then $A \cap N$ serves for $Q \cap Q'$. This is where the text's choice of representative must be adjusted: the representatives used for $Q$ and $Q'$ at $z$ may differ, and the key step moves the neighbourhood from one to the other.

*Step 4, unions*: a representative and a set $A$ for one member of the union serve for the union.

*Step 5, the sets $|\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)|$ are open.* Let $z = |\Psi_\theta(x)|$ with $x \in A \cap \theta$. By Step 1, $\Psi_\theta(x)$ is itself some $\Psi'_{\theta'}(\bar o')$; the key step applied to the isomorphism $\Psi'_{\theta'}(\bar o') \cong \Psi_\theta(x)$ and the open set $A \ni x$ gives an open $N \ni \bar o'$ with $|\Psi'_{\theta'}(N \cap \theta')| \subset |\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)|$, which is the condition of Definition 17.4.17 at $z$.

**The printed form.** *Step 1.* Under Definition 17.4.17 as printed, the condition must hold at every point of $|bC|$, so for $Q = \emptyset$ some $|\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)|$ with $\bar o \in A$ would be contained in $\emptyset$, while it contains $|\Psi_\theta(\bar o)|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.not_isBdryOpenLiteral_empty⟧. *Step 2.* The quadrant polyfold $[0,\infty)^2 \oplus W$ with the trivial group has two objects of $bC$ over the corner ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_tameBoundaryUniformizer_witness⟧, so $bC$ is non-empty and the printed collection does not contain $\emptyset$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_18_literal⟧.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The text proves that intersections are open by taking one representative for $z \in Q \cap Q'$ and appealing to the local sc-diffeomorphism $\bar f$ between representatives; the formalization makes this explicit as the key step, proved from the admissible triples and the face germs of Lemma 17.4.13, and uses it three times (intersections, the openness of $|\Psi_\theta(A)|$, and in later sections the continuity of $|\Psi_\theta|$). The two readings change no argument of the text: they are the forms in which the text's proof is written.

### XVII.44 Theorem 17.4.19 (the face uniformizers), Lemma 17.4.20 and Corollary 17.4.21 (new in the 2021 edition), pp. 803–804

**Statement** (narrower). *Lemma 17.4.20* (p. 803, (17.75)) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_20⟧: assume $\hat\Psi$ is continuous for every uniformizer ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.HatContinuous⟧ and that $G$ fixes $\bar o$ ⟦Chapter17/LocalUniformizer · GroupoidalCategory.Uniformizer.FixesPoint⟧. Then for every face $\theta$ of a good neighbourhood $U(\bar o)$, the functor $\Psi_\theta : G_\theta \ltimes \theta \to bC$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceFunctor⟧ induces an open embedding $|\Psi_\theta| : G_\theta \backslash \theta \to |bC|$, whose image is $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.range_faceFunctor_orbitMap⟧. *Corollary 17.4.21* (p. 804) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.corollary_17_4_21⟧: if $\hat\Psi$ is continuous for every uniformizer and every uniformizer is centred ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsCentred⟧, every point of $|bC|$ has an open metrizable neighbourhood. *The second half of Theorem 17.4.19* (p. 803) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.theorem_17_4_19⟧: under the same two hypotheses, for every representative with $G$ fixing $\bar o$, $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$ is open in $|bC|$, $|\Psi_\theta|$ is an open embedding, and its range is $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$. (Metrizability, the first half, is proved in the section on Propositions 17.4.23–17.4.25 below.)

*Readings and hypotheses.* (1) Theorem 17.4.19 says "a homeomorphism onto an open subset of $|C|$"; we read $|bC|$, as the clause after "i.e." does and as Lemma 17.4.20 states. (2) $\hat\Psi$ continuous (`hc`) and $G$ fixing $\bar o$ (`hfix`, supplied for every uniformizer by `hcent`) are the standing hypotheses of the face uniformizers, from Lemma 17.4.6 on. They enter through the functor $\Psi_\theta$ itself: its morphisms $\Psi_\theta(g, x) = (\Psi_\theta(x), \Psi(g, x), \Psi_\theta(g * x))$ are morphisms of $bC$ because $\hat\Psi(g, x)$ carries the face germ of $\theta$ at $x$ to that of $g(\theta)$ at $g * x$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.germLift_hat_faceGerm⟧, which uses the continuity of $\hat\Psi$, and $g(\theta)$ is again a face of $U(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.smul_mem_faces⟧, which uses that $g$ fixes $\bar o$. Without continuity of $\hat\Psi$ Lemma 17.1.9 does not hold as we read it (Findings, Chapter 17, item 1). Witnesses: the swap construction $\mathbb Z/2 \ltimes ([0,\infty)^2 \oplus W)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.swapConstruction_hatContinuous⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.swapConstruction_isCentred⟧, with a non-trivial group exchanging two faces (not weak), and the quadrant polyfold ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_hatContinuous⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.quadrant_isCentred⟧ (weak: trivial group).

**Proof of Lemma 17.4.20.** Write $\pi_\theta : \theta \to G_\theta \backslash \theta$ and $\pi : bC \to |bC|$, so that $|\Psi_\theta|(\pi_\theta(x)) = \pi(\Psi_\theta(x))$.

*Step 1, continuity.* The map $x \mapsto |\Psi_\theta(x)|$ from $\theta$ (subspace topology of $O$) to $|bC|$ is continuous: for an open $Q$ and $x$ with $|\Psi_\theta(x)| \in Q$, the openness at every point of a face (previous section) gives an open $N \ni x$ with $|\Psi_\theta(N \cap \theta)| \subset Q$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsBdryOpen.exists_nhds⟧, and $N \cap \theta$ is a neighbourhood of $x$ in $\theta$. Since $\pi_\theta$ is a quotient map, $|\Psi_\theta|$ is continuous. The text argues the same way, from a set $A(o) \subset \theta$ given at the point $o$; the corollary of the previous section is what provides such a set at every point of $\theta$, not only at the centre $\bar o$ of the representative.

*Step 2, injectivity.* If $|\Psi_\theta(x)| = |\Psi_\theta(y)|$, there is a morphism $\Phi : \Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi_\theta(y)$ in $bC$. By Lemma 17.4.6 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_6⟧ $\Psi_\theta$ is full, so $\Phi = \Psi_\theta(g, x)$ for some $(g, x)$ with $g \in G_\theta$ and $g * x = y$, and $\pi_\theta(x) = \pi_\theta(y)$. The fullness itself comes from the description of the morphisms of $bC$ between face objects: a morphism $\Psi_\theta(x) \to \Psi_{\theta'}(x')$ over $\varphi$ is $\bar\Psi(g, (x, \theta))$ with $g * x = x'$, $\Psi(g, x) = \varphi$ and $g(\theta) = \theta'$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_smul_of_bLift⟧, so $g \in G_\theta$ when $\theta' = \theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceStabilizer⟧.

*Step 3, openness.* An open set $W$ of $G_\theta \backslash \theta$ has open preimage $\pi_\theta^{-1}(W) \subset \theta$, which is $A \cap \theta$ for an open $A \subset O$ (the subspace topology). Then $|\Psi_\theta|(W) = |\Psi_\theta(A \cap \theta)|$, which is open by Lemma 17.4.18 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isOpen_faceImageOn⟧.

*Step 4.* A continuous, injective, open map is an open embedding. Its range is $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.range_faceFunctor_orbitMap⟧, and $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)| = |\Psi_\theta(O \cap \theta)|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceImage_eq_faceImageOn⟧ is open ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isOpen_faceImage⟧. This is the homeomorphism (17.75); the "obvious homeomorphism" (17.69) is this statement face by face (point G.214).

**Proof of Corollary 17.4.21.** *Step 1, $G_\theta \backslash \theta$ is metrizable.* $\theta$ is a sub-M-polyfold of $O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceSubPolyfold⟧, and $G_\theta$ acts on it by sc-diffeomorphisms, the restrictions of the action of $G$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isScSmoothMap_faceStabilizer_smul⟧. The orbit space of a finite group acting on an M-polyfold by sc-smooth maps is metrizable (Definition 17.1.2, the translation groupoid) ⟦Chapter17/GroupoidalCategory · metrizableSpace_translation_orbitSpace⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.metrizableSpace_faceOrbitSpace⟧. *Step 2.* By Lemma 17.4.20, $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$ is homeomorphic to $G_\theta \backslash \theta$, hence metrizable ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.metrizableSpace_faceImage⟧. *Step 3, every point lies in such a set.* With centred uniformizers every object of $bC$ is some $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ with $G$ fixing $\bar o$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_faceObj_center_eq_of_isCentred⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsCentred.fixesPoint⟧: the uniformizer of the tuple is centred at its object, and the fixed point is the point $\bar o$ over it, since $\Psi$ is injective on objects. Then $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$ is an open metrizable neighbourhood of $z$.

**Proof of the second half of Theorem 17.4.19.** It is the conjunction of the openness of $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$, Lemma 17.4.20 and the description of the range, for every representative with $G$ fixing $\bar o$.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The text's proof of Lemma 17.4.20 calls $|\Psi_\theta|$ "a bijection between open sets" and argues openness and continuity; the formalization proves injectivity explicitly from the fullness of Lemma 17.4.6 and states the result as an open embedding. Corollary 17.4.21 needs that every point of $|bC|$ is represented by a face of a uniformizer whose group fixes the centre; this is the centring hypothesis, which the text's phrase "$G$ the isotropy group of $\alpha$" on p. 792 presupposes (point G.195).

### XVII.45 Lemma 17.4.22 (new in the 2021 edition), p. 804, (17.76): the forgetful map $\sigma : |bC| \to |C|$

**Statement** (narrower). (17.76) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdrySigma⟧: $\sigma : |bC| \to |C|$, $|[\alpha, \Psi, U(\bar o), \theta]| \mapsto |\alpha|$, is the orbit map of the forgetful functor $B_C : bC \to C$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryFunctorC⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdrySigma_π⟧. *Lemma 17.4.22* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.lemma_17_4_22⟧: $\sigma$ is continuous ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.continuous_bdrySigma⟧, and, if $\hat\Psi$ is continuous for every uniformizer and every uniformizer is centred, $\sigma$ maps closed sets to closed sets ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isClosedMap_bdrySigma⟧. Continuity needs neither hypothesis; the lemma as a whole carries both, with the witnesses of the previous section.

**Proof of continuity.** *Step 1.* Let $Q \subset |C|$ be open and $z \in \sigma^{-1}(Q)$; write the object as $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_faceObj_center_eq⟧. *Step 2.* $A = \{q \in O : |\Psi(q)| \in Q\}$ is open, since $q \mapsto |\Psi(q)|$ is continuous, and contains $\bar o$, since $\sigma(z) = |\Psi(\bar o)|$. *Step 3.* For $x \in A \cap \theta$, $\sigma(|\Psi_\theta(x)|) = |\Psi(x)| \in Q$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceObj_α⟧. So $\Psi_\theta(\bar o)$ and $A$ witness Definition 17.4.17 for $\sigma^{-1}(Q)$ at $z$. The text takes $Q = |\Psi(W(\bar o))|$; the formalization uses the preimage directly, which avoids the reduction to such $Q$.

**Proof of closedness.** Let $A \subset |bC|$ be closed and $w \notin \sigma(A)$; we find an open neighbourhood of $w$ disjoint from $\sigma(A)$. Represent $w$ by an object $\beta$, take its reference uniformizer $\Psi = \Psi_\beta$ and the point $\bar o$ with $\Psi(\bar o) = \beta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.obj_refPt⟧; $G$ fixes $\bar o$ by centring.

*Case 1, $\bar o \in \partial O$.* *Step 1, a good neighbourhood.* $O$ is tame, so $\bar o$ has a good open neighbourhood $U(\bar o) = V(\bar o) \cap \partial O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_goodNbhd⟧. *Step 2, the face objects over $\beta$ are not in $A$.* For every face $\theta$ of $U(\bar o)$, $\sigma(|\Psi_\theta(\bar o)|) = |\beta| = w \notin \sigma(A)$, so $|\Psi_\theta(\bar o)| \notin A$. *Step 3, neighbourhoods in the faces.* $A^c$ is open, so the openness at every point of a face gives open $N_\theta \ni \bar o$ with $|\Psi_\theta(N_\theta \cap \theta)| \subset A^c$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.IsBdryOpen.exists_nhds⟧. There are $d_O(\bar o)$ faces ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.encard_faces⟧, finitely many, so $N_0 = \bigcap_\theta N_\theta$ is open. *Step 4, a $G$-invariant shrinking.* $W = V(\bar o) \cap \bigcap_{g \in G} g^{-1} N_0$ is open, $G$ being finite and acting continuously, and contains $\bar o$ because $g \bar o = \bar o$ for every $g$. This is where the centring enters. *Step 5, $|\Psi(W)|$ is disjoint from $\sigma(A)$.* It is open in $|C|$, since $q \mapsto |\Psi(q)|$ is open, and contains $w$. Suppose $z \in A$ with $\sigma(z) = |\Psi(x)|$, $x \in W$. By (17.79) (next section) $z = |\Psi_\theta(x')|$ for some face $\theta$ and $x' \in \theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_mem_faceImage⟧. Then $|\Psi(x)| = |\Psi(x')|$, so $x' = g x$ for some $g \in G$, because $|\Psi|$ is injective on $G \backslash O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_smul_eq_of_π_obj_eq⟧. Since $x \in g^{-1} N_0$, $x' \in N_\theta$, and $z \in |\Psi_\theta(N_\theta \cap \theta)| \subset A^c$, which is impossible.

*Case 2, $\bar o \notin \partial O$.* $|\Psi(O \setminus \partial O)|$ is an open neighbourhood of $w$, and no object of $bC$ lies over it: an object of $bC$ over $|\Psi(x)|$ forces $x \in \partial O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.mem_boundary_of_bdrySigma_eq⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isEmpty_bdrySigma_preimage_compl⟧, since the degeneracy index is preserved along morphisms and the objects of $bC$ lie over $\partial C$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.BObj.mem_boundarySet⟧. So it is disjoint from $\sigma(A)$.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The text proves closedness by contradiction at a point $z$ in the closure of $\sigma(A)$ and chooses one open $W \subset U(\bar o)$ with $|\Psi_\theta(\theta \cap W)| \cap A = \emptyset$ for every face. The formalization makes two points explicit: the neighbourhood must be $G$-invariant, because a point $|\Psi(x)|$ of $|C|$ is represented in $\theta$ by some $g x$, not by $x$; and $\bar o$ lies in the invariant neighbourhood because $G$ fixes it. It treats points off $|\partial C|$ separately, which the text does by observing that $z$ must lie in $|\partial C|$.

### XVII.46 Propositions 17.4.23–17.4.25 and the metrizability in Theorem 17.4.19 (new in the 2021 edition), pp. 803–809, (17.77)–(17.80)

**Statement** (narrower). *Theorem 17.4.19, first half* (p. 803) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.metrizableSpace_bdryOrbit⟧: if $\hat\Psi$ is continuous for every uniformizer and every uniformizer is centred, $(|bC|, T_{bC})$ is metrizable. *Proposition 17.4.23* (p. 805) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.proposition_17_4_23⟧: under the same hypotheses $|bC|$ is Hausdorff. *Proposition 17.4.24* (p. 805) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.proposition_17_4_24⟧: $|bC|$ is regular. *Proposition 17.4.25* (pp. 806–809) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.proposition_17_4_25⟧: $|bC|$ is paracompact. *(17.79)–(17.80)* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.metrizableSpace_bdrySigma_preimage⟧: for a good neighbourhood $U(\bar o) = V(\bar o) \cap \partial O$ with $G$ fixing $\bar o$, the preimage $\sigma^{-1}(|\Psi(V(\bar o))|)$ is metrizable, a disjoint union of finitely many open metrizable sets $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$.

*The route.* The text proves Hausdorffness, regularity and paracompactness in turn and then applies the Nagata–Smirnov theorem; its proof of paracompactness already contains the decisive fact, that every point $z$ of $|\partial C|$ has a neighbourhood $P(z)$ with $\sigma^{-1}(P(z))$ metrizable, (17.79)–(17.80). The formalization takes this fact as the centre: with $\sigma$ continuous into the metrizable $|C|$, it gives metrizability of $|bC|$ at once through the pulled-back form of Smirnov's theorem (Chapter 2, Part II), and the three propositions follow from metrizability (Findings, Chapter 17, item 8). The hypotheses are those of the previous sections, with the same witnesses.

**Proof of (17.79): $\sigma^{-1}(|\Psi(V(\bar o))|) = \bigcup_\theta |\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$.** *Step 1, inclusion "$\subset$".* Let $\sigma(z) = |\Psi(x)|$ with $x \in V(\bar o)$, $z = |a|$. Then $x \in \partial O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.mem_boundary_of_bdrySigma_eq⟧, so $x \in U(\bar o)$, and $z$ lies in $|B_C|^{-1}(|\Psi(U(\bar o))|)$. By (17.68) this set is the image of $|\bar\Psi|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.display_17_68⟧, where $\bar\Psi$ (17.65) sends $(x, \theta)$ to $\Psi_\theta(x)$, so $z = |\Psi_\theta(x')|$ for some face $\theta$ and $x' \in \theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.exists_mem_faceImage⟧. (17.68) is where continuity of $\hat\Psi$ and the fixed point enter here. *Step 2, inclusion "$\supset$".* $\sigma(|\Psi_\theta(x)|) = |\Psi(x)|$ with $x \in \theta \subset U(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.subset_bdry⟧. Both inclusions together are part of (17.83) below ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.display_17_83⟧.

**Proof of (17.80): the preimage is metrizable.** *Step 1, a lemma on covers* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · metrizableSpace_of_isOpen_cover⟧: a space covered by a family $\mathcal F$ of open metrizable sets any two of which are equal or disjoint is metrizable. *Proof.* Give $\mathcal F$ the discrete topology; it is metrizable, hence paracompact ⟦Chapter2/Metrizable · paracompactSpace_of_metrizableSpace⟧. The map $\tau$ sending a point to the member of $\mathcal F$ containing it is well defined by disjointness and continuous, since $\tau^{-1}(T)$ is the union of the members in $T$, which is open. The preimage of each singleton $\{E\}$ is $E$, metrizable. Smirnov's theorem in its pulled-back form ⟦Chapter2/Metrizable · metrizableSpace_of_pullback⟧ applies: a space with a continuous map to a paracompact Hausdorff space such that every point downstairs has an open neighbourhood with metrizable preimage is metrizable (its proof pulls back a subordinate partition of unity and glues the metrics). *Step 2, the cover.* In $Y = \sigma^{-1}(|\Psi(V(\bar o))|)$ take the traces of the sets $|\Psi_\theta(\theta)|$, $\theta \in F_{U(\bar o)}$. They cover $Y$ by Step 1 of (17.79); they are open by Lemma 17.4.20 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isOpen_faceImage⟧; any two are equal or disjoint by Lemma 17.4.8 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_8⟧; and each is metrizable by Corollary 17.4.21 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.metrizableSpace_faceImage⟧. Step 1 gives that $Y$ is metrizable. The text writes $Y$ as the finite disjoint union $\bigsqcup_{i=1}^k |\Psi_i(\theta_i)|$ over representatives of the faces modulo $G$, and as the image of $|X_U|$ under $|\bar\Psi|$ with $X_U = \bigsqcup_\theta \theta$; the cover lemma uses only that the pieces are open, metrizable and pairwise equal or disjoint, so neither the choice of representatives nor the M-polyfold $X_U$ is needed.

**Proof of metrizability (Theorem 17.4.19).** *Step 1.* $|C|$ is metrizable (Definition 17.1.1), hence paracompact Hausdorff ⟦Chapter2/Metrizable · paracompactSpace_of_metrizableSpace⟧, and $\sigma$ is continuous (Lemma 17.4.22). *Step 2, a neighbourhood at every point of $|C|$.* Let $w = |\beta|$, with the reference uniformizer $\Psi$ of $\beta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryRefUnif⟧ and $\Psi(\bar o) = \beta$; $G$ fixes $\bar o$ by centring. If $\bar o \in \partial O$, choose a good open neighbourhood $U(\bar o) = V(\bar o) \cap \partial O$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_goodNbhd⟧; then $|\Psi(V(\bar o))|$ is an open neighbourhood of $w$ and its preimage is metrizable by (17.80). If $\bar o \notin \partial O$, $|\Psi(O \setminus \partial O)|$ is an open neighbourhood of $w$ with empty, hence metrizable, preimage ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.isEmpty_bdrySigma_preimage_compl⟧. *Step 3.* The pulled-back Smirnov theorem ⟦Chapter2/Metrizable · metrizableSpace_of_pullback⟧ gives that $|bC|$ is metrizable.

**Proof of Propositions 17.4.23–17.4.25.** A metrizable space is Hausdorff and regular, and paracompact ⟦Chapter2/Metrizable · paracompactSpace_of_metrizableSpace⟧; each proposition is metrizability followed by this.

*Where Lean departs from the text.* The text's order is Hausdorff (from the Hausdorffness of $|\partial C|$ and the disjointness of the face images over one object), regular (a case analysis with (17.77)–(17.78)), paracompact (a locally finite closed cover by the $\sigma^{-1}(A_z)$ and Proposition 2.7.4), then metrizable by the Nagata–Smirnov theorem. The formalization proves metrizability first, through the one ingredient (17.79)–(17.80) of the text's proof of Proposition 17.4.25 and the pulled-back Smirnov theorem of Chapter 2, which packages the partition-of-unity argument the text performs by hand. So the closedness of $\sigma$ is not needed for metrizability; it is proved as Lemma 17.4.22 states it. The neighbourhood of a point of $|\partial C|$ is $|\Psi(V(\bar o))|$, open in $|C|$, rather than $|\Psi(U(\bar o))|$, open in $|\partial C|$; the text notes that either will do. The verdict is "narrower" because every statement of this section carries the hypotheses $\hat\Psi$ continuous and centred uniformizers.

### XVII.47 Theorem 17.4.26 (new in the 2021 edition), p. 809: the tame boundary $bC$ is a tame polyfold

**Statement** (narrower). Let $(C, F, M)$ be tame with $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$ for all $\alpha$, $\hat\Psi$ continuous for every uniformizer and every uniformizer centred. *The GCT* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryGCT⟧: $bC$, the groupoidal category of Definitions 17.4.2–17.4.4 with finite hom-sets ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryCategory · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryCategory⟧, with the metrizable topology $T_{bC}$ of Theorem 17.4.19, is a GCT. *The face uniformizers* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.FaceData⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.FaceData.unif⟧: data $d = (\Psi, \bar o, U(\bar o), \theta)$ with $G$ fixing $\bar o$ give a local uniformizer $\Psi_\theta : G_\theta \ltimes \theta \to bC$ of this GCT. *Theorem 17.4.26* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.theorem_17_4_26⟧: the polyfold construction $(F_{bC}, M)$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryConstruction⟧ on $bC$ satisfies (i) $F_{bC}(\bar\alpha) \ne \emptyset$ for every object $\bar\alpha$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.hasUniformizers_bdryConstruction⟧; (ii) $F_{bC}(\bar\alpha)$ consists exactly of the $\Psi_\theta$ for data $d$ with $\Psi \in F(\alpha)$ and $\Psi_\theta(\bar o) = \bar\alpha$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryF⟧; (iii) for $L, L' \in F_{bC}$, with the data chosen for them ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceDataOf⟧, $L = \Psi_\theta$, $L' = \Psi'_{\theta'}$, there is a bijection $\iota : M_{F_{bC}}(L, L') \to M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$ which is a local sc-diffeomorphism onto the sub-M-polyfold $M(\Psi, \Psi')^*_{\theta,\theta'}$, is sc-smooth into $M(\Psi, \Psi')$, and keeps the underlying morphism of $C$ and the objects over the source and target. *Tameness* ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.bdryConstruction_isTame⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTame · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.theorem_17_4_26_tame⟧: $(F_{bC}, M)$ is tame, and every transition M-polyfold $M_{F_{bC}}(L, L')$ is tame.

*Readings and hypotheses.* (1) The hypotheses are the standing ones of §17.4: tameness, $F(\alpha) \ne \emptyset$, $\hat\Psi$ continuous (`hc`), centred uniformizers (`hcent`). Witness: the swap construction below, with a non-trivial group and a non-empty boundary (not weak). (2) "For all possible choices of $U(\bar o)$": $F_{bC}(\bar\alpha)$ contains $\Psi_\theta$ for every $\Psi \in F(\alpha)$, every $\bar o$ over $\alpha$, every good open neighbourhood and every face with $\Psi_\theta(\bar o) = \bar\alpha$. (3) The text names the transition construction $M$ of Theorem 17.4.16; the formalization takes for $M_{F_{bC}}(L, L')$ the M-polyfold of Definition 17.4.14 for the data chosen for $L$ and $L'$. That a different choice of data for the same $L$ gives the same M-polyfold structure is not formalized (point G.215); for the faces of one good neighbourhood the data are determined by $L$ (next section).

**Proof.** *Step 1, the GCT.* $bC$ is a groupoidal category: a groupoid in which all hom-sets are finite (Definition 17.4.4). Theorem 17.4.19 supplies the metrizable topology on $|bC|$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.metrizableSpace_bdryOrbit⟧, which is all Definition 17.1.1 asks beyond that.

*Step 2, the local uniformizers.* For data $d$, the object M-polyfold is $\theta$ with its structure as a sub-M-polyfold of $O$ (Theorem 2.5.17 for the face of the face-structured open set $V(\bar o)$) ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.faceSubPolyfold⟧; the group is $G_\theta$, acting by sc-diffeomorphisms ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.isScSmoothMap_faceStabilizer_smul⟧; the functor is $\Psi_\theta$; it is injective on objects, full and faithful by Lemma 17.4.6 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryUniformizer · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_6⟧; and $|\Psi_\theta|$ is an open embedding by Lemma 17.4.20 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTopology · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.lemma_17_4_20⟧. These are the conditions of Definition 17.1.3.

*Step 3, $F_{bC}$ and the base points.* $F_{bC}(\bar\alpha)$ is the set of the $\Psi_\theta$ of (2); each is a uniformizer at $\bar\alpha$, the point $\bar o \in \theta$ being sent to $\bar\alpha$.

*Step 4, the transition M-polyfolds.* Each $L$ in some $F_{bC}(\bar\alpha)$ is $\Psi_\theta$ for some data, and the formalization fixes one such choice ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.exists_faceData⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.faceDataOf_eq⟧. For $L = \Psi_\theta$, $L' = \Psi'_{\theta'}$, the topology, the Hausdorff and paracompactness properties and the M-polyfold structure of $M(L, L')$ are those of the face transition M-polyfold of Definition 17.4.14 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryTransitionStructure · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.GoodNbhd.ftrPolyfold⟧, transported along the identity $L = \Psi_\theta$ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrMOf⟧. The structure maps of Definition 17.1.8 (source and target local sc-diffeomorphisms, unit, inverse and multiplication sc-smooth) are those of Theorem 17.4.16 ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrSrcOf⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrTgtOf⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrUnitOf⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrInvOf⟧ ⟦Chapter17/TameBoundaryPolyfold · GCT.PolyfoldConstruction.ftrMulOf⟧. This is the polyfold construction $(F_{bC}, M)$.
