**The construction is a proper covering model** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · pointedModel⟧. $E = \sqcup O_\alpha$ (17.94) and $\mathbf E = \sqcup \{\varphi\} \times O_{s\varphi}$ (17.95) with $t(\varphi, o) = \varphi * o$ are Chapter 8's source ep-groupoid of the geometric lift, and $\hat\tau$ is its functor; that $E$ is an ep-groupoid and $\hat\tau$ an sc-smooth functor with $\Gamma$ an sc-diffeomorphism is Proposition 8.5.7 (the trivial exercise). *Step 1, (17.96)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · pointedModel_fiber⟧: a point of $E$ over $o_\beta$ is some $o_\alpha$, since $\tau_\alpha$ is injective and maps $o_\alpha$ to $o_\beta$. *Step 2, the sheets*: $U(\langle\alpha, o_\alpha\rangle) = O_\alpha$, pairwise disjoint and covering $E$, and $\hat\tau = \tau_\alpha$ is an sc-diffeomorphism on the summand $O_\alpha$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · PointedGeometricLift.isScDiffeoOn_obj_summand⟧. *Step 3* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · pointed_isProperCovering⟧: $\hat\tau$ is a proper covering functor of Definition 8.5.1 (Proposition 8.5.7, or Remark 17.8.5 with the finite fibre $\{o_\alpha\}$). *Witness* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · pointedModel_reflection⟧: $A = B$ the reflection of $\mathbb R$ pointed at $0$ over the identity covering functor of $\mathbb Z/2$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · isCoveringFunctor_id_units⟧; the group acts non-trivially ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringLift · pointedModel_reflection_nondegenerate⟧.

### XVII.66 Definition 17.8.4 (2017: 17.28), Remark 17.8.5 (2017: 17.7) and Proposition 17.8.6 (new in the 2021 edition) with (17.99), pp. 831–832

**Statement.** *Definition 17.8.4* (same; p. 831) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel⟧. *Remark 17.8.5* (extra; p. 831): the two notions agree exactly when the fibre over $\bar o$ is finite ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.remark_17_8_5⟧. *Proposition 17.8.6* (same; pp. 831–832) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · proposition_17_8_6⟧ and (17.99) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · exists_connected_restriction⟧.

**Definition 17.8.4.** An sc-smooth functor $\hat\tau : E \to G \ltimes O$ into the translation ep-groupoid of a finite group, with a $G$-fixed point $\bar o$, such that (1) $\hat\tau$ is a surjective local sc-diffeomorphism on objects, (2) the points $y \in \hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$ have pairwise disjoint neighbourhoods $U(y)$ covering $E$, each mapped sc-diffeomorphically onto $O$ (with inverse $\sigma_y$), and (3) $\Gamma : \varphi \mapsto (\hat\tau(\varphi), s(\varphi))$ an sc-diffeomorphism onto $(G \ltimes O)\,{}_s\!\times_{\hat\tau} E$. *Readings.* $\hat\tau$ in (1), (2) is the object map; only the points of the fibre carry a $U(y)$, which is then open ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.isOpen_U⟧; the definition does not ask $\hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.fiber⟧ to be finite, and we read it without it. "Connected" is the connectedness of $O$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.Connected⟧. The point $y_e$ with $e \in U(y_e)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.sheetIndex⟧ is locally constant in $e$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.isLocallyConstant_sheetIndex⟧, and every fibre $\hat\tau^{-1}(x)$ is $\{\sigma_y(x)\}$, $y \in \hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.preimage_subset_image⟧.

*Witnesses, the sheet models* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel⟧: for a nonempty discrete $G$-set $S$ and a $G$-fixed $\bar o$, the projection $G \ltimes (S \times O) \to G \ltimes O$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetEp⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetFunctor⟧ with diagonal action is a model, with fibre $S \times \{\bar o\}$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_fiber⟧ and $U(s, \bar o) = \{s\} \times O$; (3) holds with inverse $((g, o), (s, o')) \mapsto (g, (s, o'))$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · isScDiffeoOn_sheetFunctor_fibredMap⟧. For $\mathbb Z/2$ acting on $\mathbb R$ by the reflection and $S = \mathbb Z/2$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_units⟧: connected ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_units_connected⟧, two sheets and a non-trivial action ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_units_nontrivial⟧; not weak.

**Remark 17.8.5** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.remark_17_8_5⟧. *Forward*: a proper covering functor of Definition 8.5.1 has finite fibres. *Backward* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.isProperCovering⟧: with $\hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$ finite every fibre is finite (it is the image of $\hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$ under $y \mapsto \sigma_y(x)$); Definition 8.5.1 (2) holds with $U(x) = O$ and $U(e) = U(y_e)$, disjoint for distinct points of one fibre because $\hat\tau$ is injective on each $U(y)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.eq_of_obj_eq⟧; (3) is Definition 17.8.4 (3). *Converse construction* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.ofIsProperCovering⟧: a proper covering functor with a $G$-fixed point and the decomposition (2) is a model. *Infinite fibres* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_int⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetModel_int_not_isProperCovering⟧: with $S = \mathbb Z$ the sheet model is a connected model whose fibre over $0$ is $\mathbb Z \times \{0\}$, so it is not a proper covering functor of Definition 8.5.1. For finite $S$ the sheet functor is one ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · sheetFunctor_isProperCovering⟧. The fibre is finite in the construction (17.93)–(17.98) and in Definition 17.8.10.

**Proposition 17.8.6** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · proposition_17_8_6⟧. *Step 1, the set* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · invariantComponent⟧: $O' = {}$ the connected component of $\bar o$ in $O^* = \bigcap_g g^{-1}U$, the largest $G$-invariant subset of $U$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · invariantCore⟧. It is open, since $O^*$ is a finite intersection of open sets and M-polyfolds are locally connected; it is connected ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · isConnected_invariantComponent⟧; and $g * O' = O'$: *reading*, the text uses paths; here $g * O'$ is connected, contains $g * \bar o = \bar o$ and lies in $O^*$, so $g * O' \subseteq O'$. *Step 2, the restriction* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.restrictModel⟧: for an open $G$-invariant $O' \ni \bar o$, $\hat\tau^{-1}(O')$ is saturated ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.isSaturated_preimage⟧ ($\hat\tau(t\psi) = g_\psi * \hat\tau(s\psi)$), so the full subcategory $E'$ on it is an ep-groupoid (Proposition 7.1.22) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.restrictSource⟧, and $\hat\tau' : E' \to G \ltimes O'$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.restrictFunctor⟧ is a model with $U'(y) = U(y) \cap \hat\tau^{-1}(O')$: (2) because an sc-diffeomorphism restricts to sub-M-polyfolds ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · MPolyfold.isScDiffeoOn_subtype⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.isScDiffeoOn_restrict_U⟧, (3) with the inverse $((g, o), e) \mapsto \Gamma^{-1}((g, \hat\tau e), e)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.isScDiffeoOn_restrict_fibredMap⟧. It is connected when $O'$ is ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · ProperCoveringModel.restrictModel_connected⟧. The paracompactness of the subspaces $O'$ and $\hat\tau^{-1}(O')$ enters as instance arguments, which always hold (subspaces of metrizable spaces). *(17.99)* is the case $U = O$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelRestrict · invariantComponent_univ⟧.

### XVII.67 Lemmas 17.8.7–17.8.9 (2017: 17.7–17.9) and the easy exercise of p. 834, pp. 832–834

**Statement.** *Lemma 17.8.7* (stronger; p. 833): $\psi \mapsto (y_{s\psi}, y_{t\psi})$ is locally constant, for every model ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.lemma_17_8_7⟧. *Lemma 17.8.8* (same; p. 833): for $O$ connected the components $\mathbf E^\varphi$, $\varphi \in \mathbf A_{\bar o}$, are pairwise disjoint and cover $\mathbf E$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.lemma_17_8_8⟧. *Lemma 17.8.9* (stronger; p. 834) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.lemma_17_8_9⟧. *The easy exercise* (same; p. 834): a connected model is isomorphic to the model of its data $A$, $B$, $\tau$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.easy_exercise⟧ (G.230 settled).

*Readings.* `hO` in Lemma 17.8.8 and in the easy exercise is the text's "Suppose further that $O$ is connected" (p. 832). $\mathbf A_{\bar o}$ is the set of morphisms between points of $\hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$. $\mathbf E^\varphi$ is the connected component of $\varphi$ in $\mathbf E$, which is also its path component.

**Lemma 17.8.7** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.lemma_17_8_7⟧: on the open set $s^{-1}(U(y_{s\psi})) \cap t^{-1}(U(y_{t\psi}))$ the pair is constant, since $s$, $t$ are continuous and the $U(y)$ are open. No connectedness is used.

**Lemma 17.8.8.** *Step 1, the group index* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.groupIndex⟧: $\hat\tau(\psi) = (g_\psi, \hat\tau(s\psi))$, so $\hat\tau(t\psi) = g_\psi * \hat\tau(s\psi)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.obj_tgt⟧ and $g_{\psi'\psi} = g_{\psi'}g_\psi$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.groupIndex_comp⟧. By the injectivity of $\Gamma$ a morphism is determined by $g_\psi$ and $s\psi$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.eq_of_groupIndex_eq⟧, and $\psi \mapsto (g_\psi, y_{s\psi})$ is locally constant ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.isLocallyConstant_groupIndex_sheetIndex⟧. *Step 2, the base morphism* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.baseMor⟧: $\varphi_\psi = \Gamma^{-1}((g_\psi, \bar o), y_{s\psi})$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.liftMor⟧ lies in $\mathbf A_{\bar o}$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.baseMor_mem⟧, since $\hat\tau(t\varphi_\psi) = g_\psi * \bar o = \bar o$. *Step 3, covering* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.mem_connectedComponent_baseMor⟧: *reading*, the text's path is replaced by the connected set $O$: $o \mapsto \Gamma^{-1}((g_\psi, o), \sigma_y(o))$, $y = y_{s\psi}$, is continuous ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.continuous_liftMor_σ⟧, so its image is connected and contains $\psi$ (at $o = \hat\tau(s\psi)$) and $\varphi_\psi$ (at $o = \bar o$). *Step 4, disjointness*: if $\mathbf E^\varphi = \mathbf E^{\varphi'}$, the locally constant $(g, y_s)$ agrees at $\varphi$ and $\varphi'$, and an element of $\mathbf A_{\bar o}$ is determined by these ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.eq_of_mem_morOn_fiber⟧. Moreover $y_s$, $y_t$ and $g$ are constant on $\mathbf E^\varphi$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.constant_on_component⟧.

**Lemma 17.8.9 and the maps $\varphi *$** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.lemma_17_8_9⟧. For $\psi \in \mathbf E^\varphi$: $g_\psi = g_\varphi$ and $y_{t\psi} = t\varphi$, so $t\psi$ is the point of $U(t\varphi)$ over $\hat\tau(t\psi) = g_\varphi * \hat\tau(s\psi)$, i.e. $t\psi = \sigma_{t\varphi}(g_\varphi * \hat\tau(s\psi))$. The identity uses only these three equalities, so it holds on every $\psi$ with the same sheet indices and group index, without connectedness. The map $\varphi * q = \sigma_{t\varphi}(g_\varphi * \hat\tau(q))$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.fiberAct⟧ is an sc-diffeomorphism $U(s\varphi) \to U(t\varphi)$ with inverse $\varphi^{-1} *$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.isScDiffeoOn_fiberAct⟧, functorial ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.fiberAct_comp⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModel · ProperCoveringModel.fiberAct_unit⟧, as $g_{\varphi'\varphi} = g_{\varphi'}g_\varphi$.

**The easy exercise** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.easy_exercise⟧. *Step 1, the data.* $\varphi \mapsto g_\varphi$ is a covering functor $\mathbf A_{\bar o} \to G$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.coveringD⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.isCoveringFunctor_coveringD⟧ ($\varphi \mapsto (g_\varphi, s\varphi)$ is a bijection onto $G \times \hat\tau^{-1}(\bar o)$, by Definition 17.8.4 (3)); $A(y) = (U(y), y)$, $A(\varphi) = \varphi *$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.modelA⟧; $B(\beta) = (O, \bar o)$, $B(g) = g *$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.modelB⟧; $\tau_y = \hat\tau|U(y)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.modelτ⟧; together a pointed lift ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.modelLift⟧. The construction needs finitely many objects: the fibre is taken finite, which is exactly when $\mathbf A_{\bar o}$ and its morphism sets are finite. *Step 2, the maps.* On objects $(y, q) \mapsto q$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.objMap⟧ with inverse $q \mapsto (y_q, q)$; on morphisms $((a, b, \varphi), q) \mapsto \Gamma^{-1}((g_\varphi, \hat\tau q), q)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.morMap⟧ with inverse $\psi \mapsto ((y_{s\psi}, y_{t\psi}, \varphi_\psi), s\psi)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.morInv⟧. *Step 3, compatibilities.* The target of the image of $((a, b, \varphi), q)$ is $\varphi * q$ by Lemma 17.8.9; this is the one place where connectedness enters ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.tgt_morMap⟧. Composition and units are preserved ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.morMap_comp⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.morMap_unit⟧ ($g$ is multiplicative), and $\hat\tau$ is respected ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.obj_objMap⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.mor_morMap⟧. *Step 4, sc-diffeomorphisms* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.isScDiffeoOn_obj⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.isScDiffeoOn_mor⟧: on each summand the maps are $\sigma_y$-charts and $q \mapsto \Gamma^{-1}((g, \hat\tau q), q)$, which is sc-smooth ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · ProperCoveringModel.isScSmoothMap_liftMor⟧. *Witness* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringModelIso · easy_exercise_units⟧: the two-sheet model of the reflection.

### XVII.68 Definition 17.8.10 (2017: 17.29) and Remark 17.8.11 (2017: 17.8): proper covering uniformizers, pp. 834–835

**Statement.** *Definition 17.8.10* (same; pp. 834–835) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer⟧. *Remark 17.8.11* (explicit; p. 835): (first part) $\Psi$ is a uniformizer centred at $\beta$ with group $G \cong G_\beta$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.toLocalUniformizer⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isotropy⟧; (second part) for a connected model, the restriction of $\hat\Psi$ to each sheet is a uniformizer of $A$ centred at the point over $\alpha$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.remark_17_8_11_two⟧.

**Definition 17.8.10.** At $\beta$: a uniformizer $\Psi : G \ltimes O_\beta \to B$ of Definition 17.1.3 without its topological condition (fully faithful, injective on objects), an ep-groupoid $E$ with a model $\hat\tau : E \to G \ltimes O_\beta$ (Definition 17.8.4), and a functor $\hat\Psi : E \to A$ such that (1) $\Psi(o_\beta) = \beta$ and $\hat\Psi$ maps $\hat\tau^{-1}(o_\beta)$ bijectively onto $P^{-1}(\beta)$, (2) $\hat\Psi$ is fully faithful and injective on objects with $P \circ \hat\Psi = \Psi \circ \hat\tau$, (3) $P^{-1}(\Psi(O_\beta))$ is the image of $\hat\Psi$, (4) $|\Psi|$ and $|\hat\Psi|$ are embeddings onto open sets $U$ and $|P|^{-1}(U)$. *Readings.* "With isotropy $G = G_\beta$" is a consequence (below); the distinguished points $o_\alpha$, $\alpha \in P^{-1}(\beta)$, are the points of $\hat\tau^{-1}(o_\beta)$; "homeomorphism onto $U$" is an embedding with that range. The definition does not use that $P$ is a proper covering functor. *Witnesses* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · sheetUniformizer⟧ (for the sheet functor: $\Psi$ tautological, $\hat\Psi$ the identity, at a $G$-fixed point) and ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · reflectionSheetUniformizer⟧: two sheets of the reflection at $0$, over a proper covering functor of GCTs, with a fibre of two points and a non-trivial action ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · reflectionSheetUniformizer_nondegenerate⟧.

**First part.** *Step 1* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.toLocalUniformizer⟧: (4) makes $|\Psi|$ an open embedding, which is the topological condition of Definition 17.1.3. *Step 2* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isCentredAt⟧: $\Psi(o_\beta) = \beta$ with $o_\beta$ fixed by $G$ (Definition 17.8.4). *Step 3* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isotropy⟧: $g \mapsto \Psi(g, o_\beta)$ is a group isomorphism onto the isotropy group of $\beta$, since $G$ fixes $o_\beta$ and $\Psi$ is fully faithful. The footprint of $\Psi$ is $U$, and $|P|^{-1}(U)$ is the image of $|\hat\Psi|$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.footprint_toLocalUniformizer⟧.

**Second part.** *Reading* `hO_conn`. We read it for a connected model, the setting of its notation $O_\alpha$, $G_\alpha \ltimes O_\alpha$ and of the action $\varphi *$ (pp. 832–834 begin "Suppose further that $O$ is connected"; Proposition 17.8.6 shrinks any model to a connected one); $O_\alpha$ is the sheet $U(y)$ of the point $y$ over $\alpha$, parametrized by $\sigma_y : O \to U(y)$. *Step 1, the group* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.sheetIsotropyHom⟧: $\varphi \mapsto g_\varphi$ is a homomorphism $G_y \to G$, injective because a morphism is determined by $g_\varphi$ and its source ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.injective_sheetIsotropyHom⟧; $G_y$ acts on $O$ through it ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.sheetGroupoid⟧. *Step 2, the sheet functor* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.sheetIncl⟧: $o \mapsto \sigma_y(o)$, $(h, o) \mapsto$ the morphism over $g_h$ with source $\sigma_y(o)$, whose target is $\sigma_y(g_h * o)$ by Lemma 17.8.9 (this uses `hO_conn`) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.tgt_liftMor_σ⟧. It is full ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.sheetIncl_full⟧ (a morphism between two points of $U(y)$ lies over an element of $G_y$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.exists_isotropy_of_mor⟧) and faithful ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.sheetIncl_faithful⟧, with image $U(y)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.range_sheetIncl_obj⟧, and an open embedding on orbit spaces ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringModel.isOpenEmbedding_orbitMap_sheetIncl⟧ (continuous, open since $\sigma_y$ and $\pi_E$ are open, injective by fullness). *Step 3* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.sheetUniformizerAt⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.sheetLocalUniformizerAt⟧: $\hat\Psi \circ$ (sheet functor) is a uniformizer, with $|\cdot|$ a composite of open embeddings ($|\hat\Psi|$ is one when $|P|$ is continuous ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isOpenEmbedding_hat_orbitMap⟧), centred at $\hat\Psi(y)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.sheetUniformizerAt_isCentredAt⟧. *Step 4* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.remark_17_8_11_two⟧: every $\alpha \in P^{-1}(\beta)$ is $\hat\Psi(y)$ for a point $y$ of the fibre (Definition 17.8.10 (1)). *Witness* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetUniformizer · remark_17_8_11_two_reflection⟧: the two-sheet uniformizer of the reflection, connected model, $G_y \cong \mathbb Z/2$ acting non-trivially at both points; not weak.

**Without connectedness.** *Step 1, an obstruction* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetCounterexample · GroupoidalCategory.not_exists_uniformizer_range⟧: a set $R$ of objects is the image of no uniformizer if it contains an object $y$ with a non-unit automorphism and an object $z$ whose only morphism into $R$ is its unit (fullness would give $g \ne 1$ over the automorphism, and $(g, o_z)$ would map to $1_z$, against faithfulness). *Step 2* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetCounterexample · TwistedSheet.model⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetCounterexample · TwistedSheet.unif⟧: $O = \{\bar o, p\}$ discrete with the trivial action of $\mathbb Z/2$, two sheets, $-1$ fixing $(s, \bar o)$ and exchanging $(a, p)$, $(b, p)$; a proper covering uniformizer with $O$ disconnected, for which $\hat\Psi(U(a, \bar o))$ is the image of no uniformizer of $A$, in the weakest sense ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetCounterexample · remark_17_8_11_two_disconnected⟧. *Step 3, effective* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetEffective · TwistedLine.model⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetEffective · TwistedLine.unif⟧: $O$ two copies of $\mathbb R$ with the reflection, the two sheets exchanged over the second copy; $G \ltimes O$ and $E$ are effective ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetEffective · isEffective_sheetEp_units⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetEffective · TwistedLine.isEffective_E⟧, and the same conclusion holds ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringSheetEffective · remark_17_8_11_two_disconnected_effective⟧. These show that the literal form, with a disconnected $O$, is the reason for the reading; connectedness is not a consequence of effectiveness.

### XVII.69 Definitions 17.8.12, 17.8.13 (2017: 17.30, 17.31), Lemma 17.8.14 (2017: 17.10) and Proposition 17.8.15 (2017: 17.12), pp. 835–838

**Statement.** *Definition 17.8.12* (same; pp. 835–836) with (17.101), (17.102) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction⟧. *Definition 17.8.13* (same; p. 836) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure⟧. *Lemma 17.8.14* (same; pp. 836–837) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.lemma_17_8_14⟧. *Proposition 17.8.15* (same; pp. 837–838) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.proposition_17_8_15⟧.

**Definition 17.8.12.** *Reading.* The functor $F : B \to \mathrm{SET}$ is read on objects, as for Definition 17.1.6; $F(\beta)$ may be empty, and the statements that need a uniformizer at $\beta$ say so (§XVII.71). The uniformizers $\Psi$ of the pairs in $F$ form a uniformizer construction of $B$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.toUniformizerConstruction⟧. *(17.101)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.HatTrans⟧: $M(\hat\Psi, \hat\Psi')$ is the set of triples $(e, \varphi, e')$ with $\varphi : \hat\Psi(e) \to \hat\Psi'(e')$. *(17.102)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.transMap⟧: $p(e, \varphi, e') = (\hat\tau(e), P(\varphi), \hat\tau'(e'))$, well defined because $P \circ \hat\Psi = \Psi \circ \hat\tau$. *Witness* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · sheetConstruction⟧: the sheet uniformizers at the $G$-fixed points; for a trivial action every $F(\beta)$ is nonempty ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · sheetConstruction_nonempty⟧.

**Definition 17.8.13.** *Reading.* "$A$ and $B$ for the induced structures become polyfolds" is read for $B$ as a polyfold structure (Definition 17.1.8) whose uniformizers are the $\Psi$ of the pairs in $F$, and for $A$ as the conditions (a)–(c) of Definition 17.1.8 for M-polyfold structures on the $M(\hat\Psi, \hat\Psi')$ (the $\hat\Psi$ are not uniformizers, Remark 17.8.11); with $p$ a local sc-diffeomorphism. A proper polyfold covering functor is a proper covering functor of GCTs with such a structure ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · ProperPolyfoldCoveringFunctor⟧. *Witnesses.* For a trivial action: the sheet construction with the tautological structures ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · sheetPolyfoldCoveringStructure⟧, and two sheets of $\mathbb Z/3 \ltimes \mathbb R$ with $F(\beta) \ne \emptyset$ for every $\beta$ and fibres of two points ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · z3PolyfoldCoveringFunctor⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · z3PolyfoldCoveringFunctor_nondegenerate⟧ (weak: trivial action). For any action, with uniformizers only at the $G$-fixed points ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringReflection · sheetFixedPolyfoldCoveringStructure⟧, and in particular two sheets of the reflection, with an effective non-trivial action ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringReflection · reflectionPolyfoldCoveringFunctor⟧. A proper covering uniformizer at $\beta$ needs $o_\beta$ fixed by $G$, so for the tautological $\Psi$ of $G \ltimes O$ only fixed points carry one; for the reflection the single uniformizer at $0$ has footprint $|B|$.

**Lemma 17.8.14** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.lemma_17_8_14⟧. The map (17.103) is $(e, \varphi, e') \mapsto ((\hat\tau e, P\varphi, \hat\tau' e'), e)$ into $M(\Psi, \Psi')\,{}_s\!\times_{\hat\tau} E$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.fibredTransMap⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.TransFibred⟧. *Step 1, injective* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.injective_fibredTransMap⟧: equal images give the same $e$ and the same $P(\varphi)$; then $\varphi$ and $\varphi_1$ have the same $(P\varphi, s\varphi)$, so they agree by Definition 17.8.1 (4); and $e'$ is determined by $\hat\Psi'(e') = t\varphi$, $\hat\Psi'$ being injective on objects. *Step 2, surjective* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.exists_fibredTransMap_eq⟧: for $(\tilde a, e)$ with $s(\tilde a) = \hat\tau(e)$, the morphism $\tilde\varphi$ of $\tilde a$ starts at $\Psi(\hat\tau e) = P(\hat\Psi e)$, so it lifts to $\varphi$ with source $\hat\Psi(e)$ (Definition 17.8.1 (4)) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.exists_lift⟧; $P(t\varphi) = \Psi'(t\tilde a)$, so $t\varphi = \hat\Psi'(e')$ for some $e'$ by Definition 17.8.10 (3), and $\hat\tau'(e') = t\tilde a$ by the injectivity of $\Psi'$. The inverse is ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringUniformizer · ProperCoveringUniformizer.fibredTransInv⟧.

**Proposition 17.8.15** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.proposition_17_8_15⟧. *Reading.* The fibred product carries Part I's fibred-product sub-M-polyfold structure of $M(\Psi, \Psi') \times E$ ($s$ and $\hat\tau$ are local sc-diffeomorphisms). *Step 1* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringPolyfold · MPolyfold.isLocalScDiffeo_fstOfFibredProduct⟧: the first projection $X\,{}_f\!\times_g Y \to X$ is a local sc-diffeomorphism when $g$ is one. *Step 2*: the map (17.103) is continuous (its components $p$ and $s$ are) and its composite with the first projection is $p$, a local sc-diffeomorphism by Definition 17.8.13; so it is a local sc-diffeomorphism. *Step 3*: it is bijective by Lemma 17.8.14, and a bijective local sc-diffeomorphism is an sc-diffeomorphism. The general form for any sc-smooth functor of ep-groupoids is in Chapter 8: $L_F$ is a local sc-diffeomorphism ⟦Chapter8/ProperCoveringFibredMap · EpFunctor.isLocalScDiffeo_fibredMap⟧, and an sc-diffeomorphism exactly when bijective ⟦Chapter8/ProperCoveringFibredMap · EpFunctor.isScDiffeo_fibredMap_iff_bijective⟧.

### XVII.70 The sc-smooth model $\hat\tau : E \to X$, the square (17.104) and the Morita equivalence, p. 838

**Statement** (same). For a family of proper covering uniformizers, $E = (\sqcup E_\lambda, \sqcup M(\hat\Psi_\lambda, \hat\Psi_{\lambda'}))$ is an ep-groupoid ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatEP⟧, $\hat\tau : E \to X$ is an sc-smooth functor ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModelFunctor⟧ and a proper covering functor ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModelFunctor_isProperCovering⟧, the square (17.104) commutes, and for covering families $\hat\Gamma$ and $\Gamma$ are equivalences with homeomorphic orbit maps. "One easily verifies" that $\hat\tau$ is a proper covering functor is written out (G.231 settled). The models of two covering families are related as Theorem 17.1.18 relates the $X_\Psi$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModel_morita⟧ (G.232).

*Readings.* "Natural pair of equivalences" is read as for $\Gamma_\Psi$ in Lemma 17.1.15. "Some kind of Morita equivalence" is read as Theorem 17.1.18 reads the comparison of the $X_\Psi$, together with the compatibility with the $\hat\tau$. A covering family is a family whose $\Psi_\lambda$ cover $|B|$; the models are built for any family.

**$E$ is an ep-groupoid.** *Step 1, the structure maps* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatGroupoid⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatObjPolyfold⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatMorPolyfold⟧: the groupoid is the pull-back of $A$ along $e_\lambda \mapsto \hat\Psi_\lambda(e_\lambda)$; source and target are local sc-diffeomorphisms and unit, inversion and multiplication sc-smooth on each piece, by the conditions of Definition 17.1.8 in Definition 17.8.13 ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.HatTr⟧. *Step 2, properness* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.exists_isProperMap_hatTr⟧: for $e \in E$ there is an open $W$ with $t : s^{-1}(\overline W) \to E'$ proper in every $M(\hat\Psi, \hat\Psi')$, from Proposition 17.1.11 for $M(\Psi, \Psi')$, a sheet of $\hat\tau$ at $e$, and the sc-diffeomorphism of Proposition 17.8.15 (which uses that $P$ is a proper covering functor) ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.exists_isProperMap_hatModel⟧. *Step 3* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatEpGroupoid⟧.

**The square (17.104).** $\hat\tau(e_\lambda) = \hat\tau_\lambda(e_\lambda)$, $\hat\tau(e, \varphi, e') = p(e, \varphi, e')$; $\hat\Gamma$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatFunctor⟧ assembles the $\hat\Psi_\lambda$ and $\Gamma$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.baseFunctor⟧ is $\Gamma_\Psi$ of (17.10) on $X$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.baseEP⟧; $P \circ \hat\Gamma = \Gamma \circ \hat\tau$ on objects and morphisms ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.square_obj⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.square_mor⟧ is $P \circ \hat\Psi = \Psi \circ \hat\tau$ on each piece. *$\hat\Gamma$ an equivalence* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatFunctor_isEquivalence⟧: full and faithful for every family (the morphisms of $E$ are those of $A$); essentially surjective for a covering family ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatFunctor_essSurj⟧: $P(\alpha) \cong \Psi_\lambda(q)$, and the lift of this isomorphism from $\alpha$ ends in $P^{-1}(\Psi_\lambda(O_\lambda)) = \hat\Psi_\lambda(E_\lambda)$. $|\hat\Gamma|$ is a homeomorphism ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.isHomeomorph_hatOrbitMap⟧, continuous and open by Definition 17.8.10 (4).

**$\hat\tau$ is a proper covering functor** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModelFunctor_isProperCovering⟧ (G.231). *Step 1, (1) and (2) summand by summand*: each $\hat\tau_\lambda$ is a proper covering functor by Remark 17.8.5 ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isProperCovering_τ⟧, its fibre being finite because $\hat\Psi_\lambda$ maps it bijectively onto the finite $P^{-1}(\beta)$; the even covering passes to disjoint unions ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · MPolyfold.evenlyCovered_sigmaMap⟧. *Step 2, (3)*: $L_{\hat\tau}$ is a local sc-diffeomorphism (Chapter 8), so (3) is its bijectivity ⟦Chapter8/ProperCoveringFibredMap · EpFunctor.isProperCovering_iff_bijective⟧. On the piece $M(\hat\Psi_\lambda, \hat\Psi_{\lambda'})$ it is the bijection of Lemma 17.8.14 followed by the inclusion of the piece $M(\Psi_\lambda, \Psi_{\lambda'})\,{}_s\!\times_{\hat\tau} E_\lambda$, and these pieces partition the fibred product ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.bijective_hatModel_fibredMap⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.isScDiffeo_hatModel_fibredMap⟧. No condition on the family is used.

**The Morita equivalence** ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModel_morita⟧. *Step 1, inclusions* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.isEquivalence_hatMap⟧: for $\mathcal U \circ \sigma$ whose base family covers, the reindexing $E_{\mathcal U \circ \sigma} \to E_{\mathcal U}$ is an sc-smooth equivalence, and $\hat\tau_{\mathcal U} \circ$ (reindexing) $=$ (base reindexing) $\circ\, \hat\tau_{\mathcal U \circ \sigma}$ strictly ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatMap_comp_hatModel⟧. *Step 2, the comparison* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatCompare⟧: the weak fibred product through $E_{\mathcal U \sqcup \mathcal U'}$, characterized by $i(\mathcal U' \to \mathcal V) \circ \hat f = i(\mathcal U \to \mathcal V)$ for every family $\mathcal V$ containing both ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatCompare_comp_incl⟧. *Step 3, for covering families* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatComparison⟧: as in Theorem 17.1.18, $\hat f$ is a generalized isomorphism, $\hat f_{c,c} = 1$, the cocycle identity holds, $|\hat\Gamma_{c'}| \circ |\hat f| = |\hat\Gamma_c|$ and $|\hat f|$ is a homeomorphism; moreover $[\hat\tau_{c'}] \circ \hat f_{c,c'} = f_{c,c'} \circ [\hat\tau_c]$ with $f$ the comparison of Theorem 17.1.18 for $B$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatComparison_comp_hatModel⟧. The strict form behind it is the zigzag $E_c \to E_{c \sqcup c'} \leftarrow E_{c'}$ over $X_c \to X_{c \sqcup c'} \leftarrow X_{c'}$ of sc-smooth equivalences with commuting squares and the three $\hat\tau$ proper covering functors ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringMorita · ProperPolyfoldCoveringStructure.hatModel_morita_strict⟧.

*Witnesses.* Two sheets of $\mathbb Z/3 \ltimes \mathbb R$ and the one-element family at $0$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · z3HatFam_isCoveringFamily⟧: an object of $E$ with a non-unit automorphism and a fibre of two points ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalModel · z3HatModel_nondegenerate⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringGlobalFunctor · z3HatModel_isProperCovering⟧ (weak: trivial action). Two sheets of the reflection ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringReflection · reflectionHatModel_nondegenerate⟧: the action is effective and non-trivial, $X$ is effective, $\hat\tau$ is a proper covering functor, $\hat\Gamma$, $\Gamma$ are equivalences with homeomorphic orbit maps, $(true, 0)$ has a non-unit automorphism over a non-unit automorphism of $0$, a morphism of $E$ moves $(true, 1)$ to $(true, -1)$, and the fibre over $0$ has two points; and the Morita equivalence for the families with one and two copies ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringReflection · reflectionHatModel_morita⟧. These are not weak.

### XVII.71 Proposition 17.8.16 (2017: 17.13): pull-backs of branched ep⁺-subcategories along proper polyfold covering functors, pp. 839–840

**Statement** (explicit). For a proper covering functor $P$ with a proper covering uniformizer construction $F$ and a branched ep⁺-subcategory $\Theta$ of $B$, $P^*\Theta = \Theta \circ P$ is a branched ep⁺-subcategory of $A$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16⟧; (1) manifold-type ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_one⟧, (2) orbifold-type ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_two⟧ and (3) tameness ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_three⟧ pass to $\Theta \circ P$, and (4) compactness of $|\mathrm{supp}\,\Theta|$ passes to $|\mathrm{supp}(\Theta \circ P)|$ under `unif_hyp` ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_four⟧. The easy exercise and the paracompactness are written out (G.233 settled).

*Readings.* "With the induced structures" is read through the uniformizers: $\Theta$ is branched on $B$ when $\Theta \circ \Psi$ is branched for the $\Psi$ of $F$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.IsBranchedBase⟧, and $\Theta'$ is branched on $A$ when $\Theta' \circ \hat\Psi$ is branched on $E$ for every $(\hat\Psi, \Psi) \in F$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.IsBranchedHat⟧. This is the local form of the text's proof $P^*\Theta = \hat\Gamma^*(\hat\tau^*(\Gamma^*\Theta))$ on one $E_\lambda$; the global model (17.104) is not needed. Tameness likewise ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.IsTameBase⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.IsTameHat⟧, with "$\alpha \in \mathrm{supp}(\Theta')$ and $\hat\Psi \in F(\alpha)$" read as $\hat\Psi \in F(\beta)$ for some $\alpha \in P^{-1}(\beta)$ with $\Theta'(\alpha) > 0$. *Hypothesis* `unif_hyp` in (4): a proper covering uniformizer at every point of $\mathrm{supp}\,\Theta$ ("we take a proper covering uniformizer around $\beta$"), the standing reading $F(\beta) \ne \emptyset$; the witness below satisfies it, and is not weak.

**The easy exercise for a proper covering functor $\tau : E \to X$ of ep-groupoids.** *Step 1, lifting* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.exists_lift⟧: a morphism $\varphi$ of $X$ with $s\varphi = \tau(e)$ is $\tau(\psi)$ with $s\psi = e$ (Definition 8.5.1 (3)). *Step 2, $|\tau|$ is proper* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isProperMap_orbitMap⟧: it is closed ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isClosedMap_orbitMap⟧ (for $x \notin \tau(\hat C)$ the evenly covered neighbourhood minus the images of the sheets meeting $\hat C$ is an open set missing $\tau(\hat C)$) and has finite fibres ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.finite_orbitMap_fiber⟧. *Step 3, branched* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isBranchedEpSubgroupoid_pullbackTheta⟧: the local branching structures of $\Theta'$ at $\tau(e)$ are moved by the local inverse of $\tau$ near $e$, as for equivalences (Theorem 11.3.3); $|\mathrm{supp}(\tau^*\Theta')| = |\tau|^{-1}(|\mathrm{supp}\,\Theta'|)$ is paracompact, as Definition 9.1.2 (1) asks and the text does not mention, because a space with a proper map onto a paracompact space is paracompact ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · paracompactSpace_of_isProperMap⟧. *Step 4*: manifold-type is preserved ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isManifoldType_pullbackTheta⟧ (two morphisms with the same source and image agree), compactness too ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isCompactBranched_pullbackTheta⟧, and tameness ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · EpFunctor.IsProperCovering.isTameBranched_pullbackTheta⟧ (a tame local branching structure at $\tau(e)$ is moved by the local inverse; its sheets keep a tame induced structure).

**Proof of the proposition.** *Step 1* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizer.comp_hat_eq_pullbackTheta⟧: $\Theta \circ P \circ \hat\Psi = \hat\tau^*(\Theta \circ \Psi)$, from $P \circ \hat\Psi = \Psi \circ \hat\tau$. *Step 2* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizer.isProperCovering_τ⟧: $\hat\tau$ is a proper covering functor (Remark 17.8.5; the fibre is finite since it maps bijectively onto $P^{-1}(\beta)$). *Step 3, branched* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16⟧: $\Theta \circ P$ is non-negative and functorial ($P$ is a functor), and on each $E$ it is $\hat\tau^*$ of a branched ep⁺-subgroupoid, branched by the easy exercise. *(1)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_one⟧: values $0$, $1$ pass; two morphisms of $A$ between points of the support with the same source and target have equal images (manifold-type of $\Theta$), so they agree by Definition 17.8.1 (4). *(2)*: values $0$, $1$ pass. *(3)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_three⟧: Step 1 and the tame easy exercise; combined with branched ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_branched_tame⟧. *(4)* ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.proposition_17_8_16_four⟧: $|\mathrm{supp}(\Theta \circ P)| = |P|^{-1}(|\mathrm{supp}\,\Theta|)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizerConstruction.orbitSupp_comp_eq_preimage⟧. *Reading*: the text's sequences are replaced by ultrafilters. An ultrafilter $\mathcal U$ on $|P|^{-1}(K)$, $K = |\mathrm{supp}\,\Theta|$ compact, has $|P|(\mathcal U) \to z = |\beta|$ with $\Theta(\beta) > 0$. Take a proper covering uniformizer at $\beta$ (`unif_hyp`); the image of $|\hat\Psi|$ is $|P|^{-1}(U)$, which lies in $\mathcal U$, so $\mathcal U = |\hat\Psi|(\mathcal V)$ for an ultrafilter $\mathcal V$ on $|E|$ with $|\Psi|(|\hat\tau|(\mathcal V)) = |P|(\mathcal U) \to |\Psi|(|o_\beta|)$ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · ProperCoveringUniformizer.orbitMap_comm⟧, hence $|\hat\tau|(\mathcal V) \to |o_\beta|$ since $|\Psi|$ is an embedding. $|\hat\tau|$ is proper, so $\mathcal V$ converges to some $c$, and $\mathcal U$ converges to $|\hat\Psi|(c) \in |P|^{-1}(K)$.

*Witnesses.* $\Theta_0 = 1_{\{0\}}$ on the reflection and the two-sheet construction ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringBranched · proposition_17_8_16_reflection⟧: branched, compact, of manifold-type, `unif_hyp` holds, value $1$ at both lifts of $0$; tame, with isotropy $\mathbb Z/2$ on the support ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · isTameBranched_reflectionTheta⟧ ⟦Chapter17/ProperCoveringTame · proposition_17_8_16_three_reflection⟧.

### XVII.72 Strong bundles over proper polyfold covering functors, (17.105) and (17.106), pp. 840–842

**Statement** (restated). The text leaves the definition of an sc-smooth strong bundle proper covering functor to the reader; it is read through models, as p. 842 indicates, and the reading settles G.234 ⟦Chapter18/MultisectionFunctors · StrongPolyfoldBundleConstruction.StrongBundleProperCovering⟧.

**The definition.** A functor $\tilde P : E' \to E$ covering a functor $P : C' \to C$ of the bases, bundle covering families of $E'$ and $E$ with their models $\bar\Gamma' : W' \to E'$, $\bar\Gamma : W \to E$ (§XVII.63), a functor $F : X' \to X$ of the base models, a map $A : W' \to W$ which is a proper strong bundle covering functor of Definition 8.5.9 over $F$, and $\bar\Gamma \circ A \cong \tilde P \circ \bar\Gamma'$ objectwise (the square (17.106) commutes up to isomorphism in $E$). *Reading.* The text says on p. 842 that the two models are strong bundles over ep-groupoids and that $A$ is a proper strong bundle covering functor in the sense of Definition 8.5.9; the definition takes exactly these data. The strong bundle uniformizer constructions over $\tilde P$ (Definitions 17.8.10–17.8.13 with strong bundle maps) are not formalized separately.

*Use and witness.* Chapter 18 uses the definition in Theorem 18.3.6: the pull-back of a structurable sc⁺-multisection functor along such a functor is structurable, by Theorem 13.4.4 in the models. The witness is the identity of the reflection bundle through the model with two copies of $\bar\Psi$ ⟦Chapter18/MultisectionFunctorsExamples · ReflectionBundle.reflIdCovering⟧, with Definition 8.5.9 for the identity; the pull-back of a non-trivial structurable functor along it is structurable ⟦Chapter18/MultisectionFunctorsExamples · ReflectionBundle.theorem_18_3_6_witness⟧. The covering is the identity, so the witness is weak.
